Abstract: Close-up rendering, zooming into a scene well beyond any training camera, is important for virtual production and interactive 3D content, yet remains an open challenge. 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) enables high-fidelity, real-time novel view synthesis, but its rendering quality degrades at close range. Recent diffusion-based methods that enhance the rendering by conditioning on reference images from the training set produce significant artifacts in this setting. We analyze this failure and identify its root cause: the scale gap between the close-up and reference views. We show that the features in reference-conditioned enhancement models are not scale-invariant, causing cross-view attention to retrieve incorrect correspondences when the same content appears at different scales, and that this mismatch cannot be corrected in latent space because the VAE encoder is not scale-equivariant. Building on this analysis we introduce MACRO, Multi-plane Attention for Closeup Render Optimization, a training-free method for high-quality close-up novel view synthesis from 3DGS. MACRO resolves the scale gap by leveraging the scene's known 3D structure: it decomposes the close-up into depth planes, crops and resizes references in image space to match the scale of each plane before encoding, and applies a depth-aware attention mask so each token attends only to scale-matched references. The method requires no architectural changes or additional training. We further contribute two new close-up novel view synthesis benchmarks, the first standardized evaluation protocol for this setting, and demonstrate state-of-the-art results on both, outperforming existing 3DGS and diffusion-based methods on both reconstruction and perceptual metrics. Project page: https://nitzanhod.github.io/MACRO

</details>


### [175] [SGF-CDNet: A Consistency-Discrepancy Graph Network over Semantic-Geometric Fused Nodes for Face Forgery Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03883)
*Jiayao Jiang,Bin Liu,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SGF-CDNet，通过语义-几何融合节点构建和一致性-差异双路径图网络来检测人脸伪造中的区域不和谐关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以捕捉伪造人脸中不同面部区域间的细微不和谐特征。

Method: 1) 构建SGF节点：深度融合面部解析的语义区域与地标的几何信息；2) 双路径CD-GNN：一致性路径评估生物自然模式，差异路径挖掘伪造引入的结构张力与特征冲突。

Result: 在公开数据集上取得了优越性能。

Conclusion: SGF-CDNet通过捕捉面部组件间的不和谐关系，成为可靠的人脸伪造检测方案。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of deepfakes necessitates robust face forgery detection. Although forged faces may lack obvious artifacts, they often contain subtle disharmony among different facial regions. We propose SGF-CDNet, a Consistency-Discrepancy Graph Network (CD-GNN) over Semantic-Geometric Fused (SGF) nodes. First, SGF-CDNet constructs SGF nodes by deeply fusing semantic regions from face parsing with geometric information from facial landmarks, allowing nodes to capture both high-level concepts and precise geometric constraints. Next, a dual-path CD-GNN performs parallel relational reasoning on these nodes across two dimensions: consistency and discrepancy. The consistency path evaluates if facial components follow natural biological patterns, while the discrepancy path mines for structural tensions and feature conflicts introduced by forgeries. By integrating these processes, our model effectively identifies disharmonious relationships between facial components. Extensive experiments on public datasets demonstrate that SGF-CDNet achieves superior performance, establishing it as a reliable solution for face forgery detection.

</details>


### [176] [BAT3R: Bootstrapping Articulated 3D Reconstruction from 2D Image Collections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03891)
*Jakub Zadrozny,Oisin Mac Aodha,Hakan Bilen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种利用单张图片进行3D articulated物体重建的训练框架，仅需每类一个rigged规范网格和未标注2D图像集合，通过迭代估计关节姿态和相机视角来生成训练数据，无需手动制作大量3D关节资产，性能与需要人工精心策划数据集的方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于点图的方法依赖人工创建的3D关节资产和精心设计的姿态分布，生成逼真的物体关节姿态成本高且劳动密集，本文旨在减少这一需求。

Method: 从规范姿态渲染训练的弱3D形状预测器出发，通过将网格拟合到预测的点图来迭代估计物体关节姿态和相机视角，利用恢复的关节和视角渲染更新的合成训练数据，逐步改进预测器。

Result: 尽管使用了明显更弱的3D监督，该模型达到了与需要人工策划关节训练数据集的方法（DualPM）相当的性能。

Conclusion: 该方法显著降低了3D articulated物体重建对人工制作3D资产的需求，仅需每类一个rigged规范网格和未标注2D图像即可实现强性能。

Abstract: 3D reconstruction of articulated objects from a single image is challenging because large training datasets with paired image and 3D supervision are difficult to obtain. Recent point map-based methods achieve strong performance but rely on synthetic datasets rendered from manually created articulated 3D assets with carefully curated pose distributions. While camera viewpoints can be easily sampled, generating realistic object articulations remains costly and labor-intensive. We propose a training framework that reduces this requirement by leveraging unannotated 2D images collections with only a single rigged canonical mesh per category. Starting from a weak 3D shape predictor trained on canonical-pose renders, we iteratively estimate object articulation and camera pose by fitting the mesh to predicted point maps. The recovered articulations and viewpoints are then used to render updated synthetic training data, progressively improving the predictor. Despite using substantially weaker 3D supervision, our models achieve performance comparable with DualPM, which requires manually curated articulated training datasets.

</details>


### [177] [DICT: Data Injection and Contrastive Trajectory Refinement for Conditional Image Generation with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03899)
*Chunnan Shang,Xin Zhang,Zhizhong Wang,Hongwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DICT，一种无需训练的推理方法，通过数据注入和对比轨迹精炼，在统一框架下提升条件图像生成的质量与跨任务泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有条件图像生成方法分为两类：任务特定设计（提升性能但限制泛化）和无训练损失引导（将丰富条件压缩为标量目标，导致信息瓶颈和误差累积）。亟需一个统一且有效的框架来覆盖多样化条件图像生成任务。

Method: DICT包含两个核心组件：1) 数据注入：将噪声扰动的条件信号集成到早期去噪阶段，通过引导去噪自适应选择和蒸馏任务关键信息，保留空间丰富性；2) 对比轨迹精炼：在相邻去噪状态间进行成对比较，逐步提升样本质量。整体方法无需训练，不引入任务相关架构。

Result: 在风格迁移、图像超分辨率和图像去模糊等条件图像生成任务上，DICT在保真度和感知质量上均持续优于代表性的任务特定方法和损失引导基线方法。

Conclusion: DICT通过统一扩散公式下的数据注入和对比轨迹精炼，实现了简单推理与跨任务迁移能力的提升，为条件图像生成提供了一种有效的统一框架。

Abstract: Diffusion models have become a dominant paradigm for conditional image generation, yet existing approaches generally follow two directions: task-specific designs that can improve performance but limit generalization, and training-free loss guidance that compresses rich conditions into scalar objectives and applies stepwise guidance, leading to information bottlenecks and error accumulation along the sampling trajectory. Given the urgent need for an effective unified framework across diverse conditional image generation tasks, we propose Data Injection and Contrastive Trajectory Refinement (DICT), a training-free inference method that enhances conditional image generation without introducing task-dependent architectures. DICT introduces Data Injection, where noise-perturbed conditional signals are integrated into early denoising stages; by performing guided denoising on these injected signals, DICT adaptively selects and distills task-salient information from the raw condition, effectively preserving spatial richness and ensuring precise condition-to-generation alignment. Furthermore, DICT applies Contrastive Trajectory Refinement across adjacent denoising states, enabling pairwise comparisons that progressively improve sample quality. These designs keep inference simple while improving cross-task transfer under a unified diffusion formulation. Extensive experiments on conditional image generation tasks (e.g., style transfer, image super-resolution, and image deblurring) show consistent gains in fidelity and perceptual quality over representative task-specific and loss-guided baselines.

</details>


### [178] [USE: A Unified Self-Ensembling Framework for Test-Time Prompt Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03900)
*Siru Jiang,Jian Liang,Ran He,Tieniu Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了测试时提示调优（TPT）方法，揭示其本质是从自生成伪标签中隐式学习，并提出了统一自集成框架（USE），通过优化与推理阶段的一致性以及自适应地强调测试图像本身而非其增强视图来获得更可靠的伪标签，显著提升了CLIP模型在下游任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视TPT方法，揭示其优化本质是从自生成伪标签中隐式学习，并解决其在伪标签可靠性方面的不足。

Method: 1) 提出统一自集成框架(USE)，确保优化与推理阶段一致性；2) 在优化阶段引入自集成(SE)策略，自适应地强调测试图像本身而非其增强视图以获得更可靠伪标签；3) 将相同策略应用于推理阶段以充分利用增强的潜力；4) SE也可作为轻量级免优化TTA方法。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，SE和USE分别优于对应基线方法，且SE与现有TTA方法集成时能带来一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过揭示TPT的伪标签学习本质并提出统一自集成框架，实现了优化与推理阶段的一致性，为CLIP测试时自适应提供了更有效且通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) has emerged as a popular paradigm for improving the performance of vision-language models (e.g., CLIP) on downstream tasks. Among existing CLIP-based TTA methods, Test-Time Prompt Tuning (TPT) is a pioneering work that optimizes textual prompts using multiple test-time augmentations and remains a strong baseline to date. In this work, we revisit TPT and reveal that its optimization can be interpreted as implicitly learning from self-generated pseudo labels. Building on this perspective, we propose a unified self-ensembling framework (USE) that ensures consistency between the optimization and inference stages. During optimization, we introduce a simple yet effective self-ensembling (SE) strategy that emphasizes the test image itself over its augmented views adaptively to obtain more reliable pseudo labels. To fully exploit the potential of augmentations, we further apply the same strategy at inference time, unifying the objectives of both stages. Notably, SE can also act as a lightweight optimization-free TTA method. Extensive experiments across multiple datasets demonstrate that SE and USE outperform their counterparts, respectively. Furthermore, SE yields consistent performance gains when integrated with existing TTA methods. The code is available at https://github.com/sirujiang/USE.

</details>


### [179] [NavEYE: Vision-Centered Multi-Sensor Fusion-Based Situational Awareness System for Intelligent Surface Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03915)
*Ryan Wen Liua,Junxiong Lianga,Haoyu Wanga,Mengwei Baoa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为NavEYE的以视觉为中心的多传感器融合系统，通过结合AIS、雷达和RGB相机，实现智能水面车辆在复杂航行环境中的高质量情境感知。系统包括多约束门控数据关联方法（MCGA）、距离感知自适应加权融合（DAWF）和时效衰减拼接融合（TDSF），并基于归一化方位和距离特征的联合约束进行视觉与多传感器数据的关联融合。实验结果表明该系统在定量和定性评估中均表现出优越性能，能够有效提升ISV的航行安全性和情境感知能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能水面车辆（ISV）在复杂航行环境中的情境感知能力直接影响其智能性、可靠性和安全性，而单一传感器难以满足高质量感知需求，因此需要多传感器融合方法来充分利用不同传感器的互补优势。

Method: 提出NavEYE系统：①多约束门控数据关联方法（MCGA）匹配低时间分辨率的AIS数据和雷达数据；②距离感知自适应加权融合（DAWF）和时效衰减拼接融合（TDSF）处理数据丢失问题；③基于归一化方位和距离特征的联合约束，将AIS、雷达与视觉检测结果关联融合。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的广泛实验证明，该融合方法在定量和定性评估中均表现出优越性能，基于船载的NavEYE系统能够有效提升复杂动态环境中的航行安全性。

Conclusion: NavEYE多传感器融合系统能够自动获取感兴趣船舶的综合信息，增强ISV的情境感知能力并降低碰撞风险，在复杂动态环境中有效促进航行安全。

Abstract: With the rapid development of sensor and artificial intelligence (AI) technologies, intelligent surface vehicles (ISVs) have gained increasing attention from academia and industry. Their intelligence, reliability, and safety depend heavily on situational awareness in complex navigational environments. To achieve high-quality perception, we develop a vision-centered multi-sensor fusion system, named NavEYE, by exploiting complementary sensors, including the automatic identification system (AIS), radar, and RGB camera. Specifically, we first propose a multi-constrained gated data association method (MCGA) to accurately match low-temporal-resolution AIS data with high-temporal-resolution radar data. Their fusion result is then obtained by selectively implementing distance-aware adaptively weighted fusion (DAWF) and timeliness decay-based stitching fusion (TDSF), which reduce the uncertainty caused by AIS or radar data loss in real-world sensing scenarios. Based on accurate and robust visual object detection, we further associate and fuse AIS, radar, and visual data through joint constraints of normalized bearing and distance features. According to the fusion results, comprehensive information related to ships of interest can be automatically obtained, helping enhance situational awareness and reduce collision risk for ISVs. The feasibility, robustness, usability, and effectiveness of the proposed multi-sensor fusion method and situational awareness system are demonstrated through extensive experiments on a real-world sensing dataset collected from AIS, radar, and camera. The experimental results show the superior performance of our fusion method in both quantitative and qualitative evaluations. In addition, the shipboard NavEYE system can promote navigational safety for ISVs in complex and dynamic environments.

</details>


### [180] [EgoInertia-MI: A Multimodal Egocentric Vision and IMU Benchmark for Motor Impairment Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03934)
*Fatemah Alhamdoosh,Pietro Pala,Abduallah Mohamed,DK Arvind*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了EgoInertia-MI，一个结合第一人称视频和可穿戴IMU信号的 multimodal 基准数据集，用于运动障碍评估。包含19个上下肢活动、三种严重等级，实验表明多模态融合在动作识别（0.93 Macro-F1）和严重程度估计（0.78 Macro-F1）上表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统临床评估间歇性且难以捕捉运动行为的细微时间变化；可穿戴IMU和第三人称视频虽有前景但存在隐私问题和采集限制；第一人称视觉提供更自然且保护隐私的替代方案，但目前缺乏相关多模态基准数据集。

Method: 构建EgoInertia-MI数据集，同步采集第一人称视频和IMU信号，包含19种上下肢活动、三种严重等级（无、轻度、重度）。设置动作识别和运动障碍严重程度估计两个基准任务，评估多种单模态和多模态基线方法。

Result: 多模态融合在严重程度估计上达到0.78 Macro-F1，在动作识别上达到0.93 Macro-F1，优于单一模态方法。

Conclusion: 第一人称视频为运动障碍评估提供强线索，多模态融合可实现最佳性能，表明结合第一人称视觉与可穿戴感知在生态效度和隐私保护的运动评估中具有潜力。

Abstract: Motor impairments, including tremor, bradykinesia, gait abnormalities, and postural instability, are common across many neurological and movement-related conditions. Conventional clinical assessments are often intermittent and may fail to capture subtle temporal variations in motor behavior. While wearable IMUs and third-person video have shown promise for objective motor assessment, third-person recordings raise privacy concerns and require constrained acquisition setups. In contrast, egocentric vision provides a more naturalistic and privacyaware alternative. In this work, we introduce EgoInertia-MI, a multimodal benchmark dataset combining synchronized egocentric video and wearable IMU signals for motor impairment analysis. The dataset contains 19 upper- and lower-body activities performed by healthy volunteers simulating varying levels of motor impairment severity levels: no impairment, mild impairment, and severe impairment. We establish two benchmark tasks: action recognition and motor impairment severity estimation, and evaluate multiple unimodal and multimodal baselines. Experimental results show that egocentric video provides strong cues for motor impairment assessment, while multimodal fusion achieves the best overall performance, reaching 0.78 Macro-F1 for severity estimation and 0.93 Macro-F1 for action recognition. These findings highlight the potential of combining egocentric vision and wearable sensing for ecologically valid and privacy-aware motor assessment. Code and data are available at:https://fatemah-alh.github.io/EgoInertia-MI-Page/.

</details>


### [181] [A Large-Scale Dataset and a New Method for RemoteSensing Traffic Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03945)
*Zhigang Yang,Huiguang Yao,Linmao Tian,Qiang Li,Qi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文构建了NWPU-Traffic大规模交通对象分割数据集，涵盖4类目标（汽车、飞机、轮船、火车），来自7国49个城市，并提出了基于空间-通道保持特征交互与自适应特征解码器的分割方法，实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感图像分割数据集在目标类别和场景多样性上不足，限制了模型对真实场景交通能力的全面评估，因此需要构建大规模、多样化的交通对象分割数据集。

Method: 构建NWPU-Traffic数据集（4类交通目标、7国49城、实例级标注）；提出空间-通道保持特征交互与自适应特征解码器的分割网络。

Result: 在NWPU-Traffic数据集上使用多种主流分割网络建立了基准，并通过大量实验和消融研究验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: NWPU-Traffic数据集填补了现有数据集在分辨率和场景多样性方面的关键缺陷，所提分割方法在复杂场景下表现鲁棒，数据集和代码已开源。

Abstract: Remote sensing imagery plays a crucial role in evaluating regional transportation capacity. However, existing segmentation datasets often lack diversity in object categories and scenes, limiting the ability of models to comprehensively evaluate trans portation capacity in real-world scenes. To alleviate this gap, we construct a large-scale and diverse dataset for transportation object segmentation, named as NWPU-Traffic. This dataset encompass four traffic object categories (car, airplane, ship, and train) and a wide range of scenes from 49 cities across 7 countries, with instance-level annotations to ensure precise segmentation of individual objects, which bridges critical shortcomings in resolution and scene diversity in existing datasets. Leveraging this dataset, we establish a benchmark with several popular segmentation networks. Furthermore, we propose a novel segmentation method that leverages spatial-channel preserving feature interaction and an adaptive feature decoder, enabling robust segmentation across varying scales and complex environments. Extensive experiments and ablation studies validate the effectiveness of our approach. The dataset and code are publicly available at https://github.com/CVer-Yang/NWPU-Traffic.

</details>


### [182] [TESSERA v2: Scaling Pixel-wise Earth Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03949)
*Zhengpeng Feng,Sadiq Jaffer,Ira Shokar,Jovana Knezevic,Mark Elvers,Clement Atzberger,Robin Young,Aneesh Naik,Niall Robinson,Andrew Blake,David Coomes,Anil Madhavapeddy,Srinivasan Keshav*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文对像素级地球观测基础模型的缩放规律进行了最大规模的控制性研究（395次训练，1,024个GH200芯片），发现预训练损失几乎无法预测下游性能（|r|<0.2），据此提出了计算分配规则（编码器与数据同步增长，投影器固定），并训练了0.5B/1B/2B模型系列，通过蒸馏得到21M参数的TESSERA v2-1B-M，其聚合性能超越所有开源及专有模型，且支持Matryoshka表示（16维前缀保留92%性能，节省7/8存储）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素级地球观测基础模型已通过空间嵌入取得优异性能，但其缩放规律和预训练预算分配策略尚不明确，现有研究缺乏大规模系统性的控制实验来指导计算资源的最优配置。

Method: 在1,024个GH200超级芯片上进行了395次受控训练实验，采用固定像素级Barlow Twins架构，在15个下游任务上评估；提出编码器与数据同步增长、投影器固定的计算分配规则；训练0.5B/1B/2B模型后蒸馏为紧凑学生模型，并引入Matryoshka表示学习。

Result: 预训练损失与下游性能相关性极低（|Pearson r|<0.2）；21M参数的蒸馏模型TESSERA v2-1B-M聚合性能超越所有开源和专有模型；16维Matryoshka前缀可保留92%的128维全量性能，存储成本降至1/8。

Conclusion: 为像素级EO基础模型提供了经验驱动的缩放方案：训练大编码器、以下游性能而非损失选择模型、蒸馏为灵活的学生模型，并计划发布2017-2025年的v2全局嵌入。

Abstract: Pixel-wise Earth-observation (EO) foundation models are now achieving state-of-the-art performance via generated spatial embeddings. However, how these models scale and how best to spend a pretraining budget remain poorly understood. We present the largest controlled scaling study for EO to date: 395 training runs on 1,024 GH200 superchips within a fixed pixel-wise Barlow Twins family, each evaluated on 15 downstream tasks. We find that pretraining loss barely predicts downstream performance (|Pearson r| < 0.2), so selecting models by loss wastes a large share of the compute. We also find that, as the training budget grows, the encoder and the data should grow together while the projector stays fixed, which gives a simple rule for allocating compute. Using this rule, we train a family of pixel-wise models (0.5B and 1B, with a 2B model in training) and distill them into compact students for embeddings-as-data deployment. The 21-million-parameter distilled TESSERA v2-1B-M in aggregate outperforms all open and proprietary models tested, some of which are orders of magnitude larger. These students produce Matryoshka representations that are inexpensive to serve: a 16-dimensional prefix keeps 92% of the full 128-dimensional performance at 1/8 of the storage. Upon completion of training we plan to release v2 global embeddings covering 2017-2025. Together, these results give a concrete, empirically grounded recipe for scaling pixel-wise EO foundation models: train large encoders, select by downstream performance, and distil into flexible student models. All code will be released at https://github.com/ucam-eo/tessera.

</details>


### [183] [Reward Lightning: Fast Video Generation via Homologous Preference Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03960)
*Jiaxiang Cheng,Bing Ma,Xuhua Ren,Kai Yu,Peng Zhang,Tianxiang Zheng,Qinglin Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Reward Lightning框架，通过同源表征（在相同潜空间上评估偏好对齐和蒸馏加速）统一优化视频扩散模型的两大目标，实现1-4步生成高质量视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在不相匹配的表征空间上优化偏好对齐和蒸馏加速两个目标，导致改进一个目标往往会损害另一个目标，存在梯度冲突问题。

Method: 1) 提出潜空间奖励模型(LRM)，直接在潜空间对视频评分，无需解码回像素空间；2) 基于LRM提出同源偏好蒸馏(HPD)，复用共享骨干网络联合进行对抗性蒸馏和偏好对齐。

Result: LRM在偏好准确率上超越像素级和潜空间级奖励基线11.0%和14.7%；Reward Lightning在1-4步内生成高保真视频，平均VBench分数提升2.1%，在文本对齐、运动质量和视觉质量方面均领先。

Conclusion: 通过在共享潜空间中统一优化偏好对齐和蒸馏加速，可以有效解决梯度冲突，实现高质量、高效率的视频生成。

Abstract: Achieving simultaneous preference alignment and distillation acceleration in video diffusion models remains an open challenge. Existing methods optimize the two objectives over mismatched representation spaces, where improving one objective often compromises the other. To overcome this, we propose Reward Lightning, a unified framework that aligns and accelerates a video diffusion model within a single shared representation. Its central principle is homology: both objectives are evaluated on identical latent features, which mitigates the gradient conflicts that arise when they are optimized over disjoint representations. As a foundational component, we first introduce a latent reward model (LRM) that scores videos directly in the latent space, without decoding back to the pixel space. Building on the LRM, homologous preference distillation (HPD) reuses this shared backbone to perform adversarial distillation and preference alignment jointly, yielding few-step generators that remain faithful and well aligned. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the LRM surpasses pixel-level and latent-level reward baselines by $11.0\%$ and $14.7\%$ in preference accuracy, and that Reward Lightning generates high-fidelity videos in merely $1$ to $4$ steps, improving the average VBench score by $2.1\%$ while leading in text alignment, motion quality, and visual quality. Project page: https://reward-lightning.github.io.

</details>


### [184] [DS-SAC: Density Search for Sample Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03972)
*Suraj Thapa,Muhammad Aminul Islam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DS-SAC，一种确定性鲁棒估计框架，通过搜索密集区域替代随机采样，具有多项式复杂度，在单应性、基础矩阵和本质矩阵估计任务上，比RANSAC等主流方法取得更高AUC、更低位姿误差和更快运行速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RANSAC及其变体依赖随机最小采样，存在随机性和效率问题，需要一个确定性的高效鲁棒估计方法。

Method: 方法包括：从初始模型出发进行前向/后向局部探索；利用符号残差递归划分点集，在有效分区中搜索高一致性模型。

Result: 在单应性、基础矩阵和本质矩阵估计的大规模真实数据集上，DS-SAC相比RANSAC、MAGSAC、LO-RANSAC、GC-RANSAC等方法，获得更高AUC分数、竞争性或更低的位姿中值误差，以及更快的运行时间。

Conclusion: DS-SAC作为一种确定性、多项式复杂度的鲁棒估计框架，是随机一致性方法的高效替代方案。

Abstract: Robust geometric model estimation is a fundamental problem in computer vision. RANSAC and its variants remain widely used for this task; however, they rely on stochastic minimal sampling. In this article, we propose Density Search Sample Consensus (DS-SAC), a deterministic robust estimation framework, that avoids repeated random sampling by searching dense regions. Starting from an initial model estimated from the available points, the method performs local exploration via forward and backward search. To facilitate global exploration, DS-SAC recursively partitions the point set using signed residuals and searches each valid partition for high-consensus models. We show that DS-SAC has polynomial complexity with respect to the number of points, making it an efficient alternative to stochastic consensus-based methods. Experiments on large-scale real-world datasets for homography, fundamental matrix, and essential matrix estimation show that DS-SAC achieves higher AUC scores, competitive or lower median pose errors, and faster runtime compared with widely used robust estimators, including RANSAC, MAGSAC, LO-RANSAC, and GC-RANSAC.

</details>


### [185] [InSpace: Structure-Aware 3D Indoor Scene Generation from a Single 360° Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03990)
*Gwanhyeong Koo,Hyunsu Kim,Youngji Kim,Taejae Lee,Siwoo Lim,Sunjae Yoon,Suyong Yeon,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InSpace是一个基于360°全景图（ERP表示）的结构感知3D室内场景生成框架，通过三阶段流程（部分几何估计、粗结构生成、全局-局部混合注意力生成细粒度布局与资产）实现从单张ERP图像生成完整3D室内场景及其独立带纹理资产，并提出了ERP-FRONT数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单图转3D方法主要集中在资产级生成，忽略了室内场景的结构布局信息，而结构布局对于下游应用和资产空间定位至关重要。单张普通图像视野有限，无法恢复连贯的全局布局。

Method: 提出InSpace框架，包含三阶段：(1) 估计部分场景几何作为空间先验；(2) 使用视图选择性交叉注意力生成粗场景结构；(3) 通过全局-局部混合注意力与流匹配（flow matching）生成细粒度布局和带纹理的资产几何。同时提出了基于3D-FRONT的成对ERP图像到3D室内场景数据集ERP-FRONT。

Result: InSpace能够从单张ERP图像生成完整的3D室内场景及其结构布局，以及分离的带纹理资产，在3D和2D指标上均取得了强劲性能。

Conclusion: 使用360°全景图（ERP）可以有效解决单图视野受限的问题，结合结构感知的三阶段生成框架，能够实现高质量的3D室内场景生成，同时保持结构布局的完整性和资产的可分离性。

Abstract: Recent advances in single image-to-3D generation have enabled high-quality asset synthesis, yet extending these capabilities to indoor scene generation remains challenging. Existing methods focus on asset-level generation while neglecting the structural layout, which is essential for downstream applications and serves as the spatial anchor for grounding assets. However, a single image with a limited field of view lacks the spatial coverage to recover a coherent global layout. To this end, we use a 360° image represented in equirectangular projection (ERP) and propose InSpace, a structure-aware framework for 3D indoor scene generation. InSpace comprises three stages: (1) estimating partial scene geometry as spatial priors, (2) generating coarse scene structure with view-selective cross-attention, and (3) producing detailed layout and asset geometry with textures through a global-local hybrid attention, using flow matching. We also propose ERP-FRONT, a paired ERP-Image-to-3D indoor scene dataset based on 3D-FRONT. Experiments show that InSpace generates complete 3D indoor scenes with structural layout, along with separate textured assets from a single ERP image, achieving strong performance across 3D and 2D metrics. Project Page: https://kookie12.github.io/InSpace-Project-Page/

</details>


### [186] [Full Glyph Images Beat Token Embeddings: A Controlled Study for Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03994)
*Shuyang Xiang,Hao Guan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文用视觉编码（字符光栅图像）完全替代中文语言模型中的离散token嵌入，在共享解码器架构下，视觉模型以0.429 vs 0.355的准确率超越索引基线（相对提升21%），收敛速度减半，表明离散分词并非中文语言建模的必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战语言模型中将文本表示为离散token嵌入这一根深蒂固但鲜少被质疑的假设，探索视觉输入是否可作为中文语言建模的有效替代表示。

Method: 构建双分支受控框架：视觉分支将字符序列渲染为单张光栅图像，经共享ResNet+浅层Vision Transformer编码；索引分支使用标准离散token嵌入。两分支共享完全相同的解码器主干、训练目标、优化器和数据课程，将性能差异仅归因于输入模态。

Result: 视觉模型在所有测试解码器上持续超越基线，峰值准确率0.429 vs 0.355（相对提升21%），训练epoch数减半；优势在前期（前5个epoch/总数据21%以内）即显现；字符适度损坏下视觉模型仍匹配干净基线；跨脚本实验表明优势不直接迁移到英语等字母文字系统。

Conclusion: Transformer比普遍假设的更模态无关，离散分词不是中文语言建模的基本要求；中文汉字均匀的视觉密度和部首结构是视觉优势得以存在的使能条件。

Abstract: Modern language models generally represent text as sequences of discrete token embeddings, an assumption deeply rooted in current practice but rarely questioned. We challenge this representation, especially for Chinese, by replacing index-based token embeddings entirely with a single rasterized image of the character sequence, processed by a vision encoder composed of a shared ResNet and a shallow Vision Transformer. To isolate the role of input representation, we construct a dual-branch controlled framework in which both a Vision-based model and an index-based baseline share an identical decoder backbone, training objective, optimizer, and data curriculum. Any performance difference is therefore attributable to the input modality only. Across all tested decoder backbones, the Vision-based model consistently outperforms the baseline, reaching a peak accuracy of 0.429 versus 0.355 for the index-based baseline,that is, a 21% relative improvement, while converging in about half the number of training epochs. The advantage emerges especially within the first five epochs (under 21% of total data) and persists under moderate character corruption: the corrupted Vision model matches the clean index-based baseline. Ablation studies reveal that the advantage requires both spatially coherent input and a ViT encoder with 2D positional encodings. A cross-script comparison on English shows the advantage does not transfer directly to alphabetic writing systems, suggesting that the uniform visual density and radical structure of Chinese characters are enabling conditions. These findings suggest that transformers are more modality-agnostic than commonly assumed, and that discrete tokenization is not a fundamental requirement for Chinese language modeling.

</details>


### [187] [SAGE: Synchronized Action-Gaze Recognition and Anticipation for Human Behavior Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04017)
*Chenyi Kuang,Nakul Agarwal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出统一框架SAGE，通过Transformer架构同时识别与预测人类物体交互（HOI）和注视行为，在三个数据集上超越现有专门化模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型将注视和动作分开处理，忽略了二者之间的相互依赖关系及统一解决方案的优势。

Method: 基于Transformer架构，将注视数据融入时空注意力机制，构建端到端可训练的统一模型SAGE，同时识别和预测当前及未来的HOI与注视行为，并适配第一人称（egocentric）和第三人称（exocentric）视角。

Result: 在VidHOI、EGTEA Gaze+和新建立的Exo-Cook数据集上，联合建模注视与动作在现有和未来帧上持续取得强结果，常超越各任务专门化的SOTA模型。

Conclusion: 统一建模注视与动作的框架为人机交互提供了更直观的基础，具有广泛的应用潜力。

Abstract: Human object interaction (HOI), gaze pattern, and their anticipation are intricately linked, providing valuable insights into cognitive processes, intentions, and behavior. However, most existing models handle gaze and actions separately, missing both their interdependence and the advantages of a unified solution. This paper presents a novel unified framework, SAGE (Synchronized Action-GazE), which integrates simultaneous recognition and anticipation of both HOI and human gaze into a single unified end-to-end trainable model. Our approach leverages a transformer-based architecture and incorporates gaze data into spatiotemporal attention mechanisms to simultaneously predict current and future human actions and gaze behavior. We explore this bidirectional relationship between gaze and actions under different scenarios, whether requiring a close-up, detailed view (egocentric) or a wider, more contextual view (exocentric), making our framework versatile for various applications. Additionally, due to lack of datasets for comprehensive analysis of both HOI and gaze in exocentric videos, we establish a new benchmark Exo-Cook to facilitate further research in this domain. Experiments on three benchmark datasets: VidHOI, EGTEA Gaze+, and Exo-Cook show that jointly modeling gaze and actions across current and future frames achieves consistently strong results, often surpassing specialized state-of-the-art models tailored to individual tasks. By unifying actions and attention in a comprehensive way, our work lays the groundwork for more intuitive human-machine interaction.

</details>


### [188] [Paired Uterine Whole-Slide Images and Pathology Reports for Multimodal Computational Pathology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04020)
*Han Li,Jingsong Liu,Ayako Ura,Junlin Hou,Zhengyang Xu,Azar Kazemi,Oskar Thaeter,Christian Grashei,Fabian Gülhan,Reza Nasirigerdeh,Xun Ma,Rui Yan,Hao Chen,S. Kevin Zhou,Nassir Navab,Carolin Mogler,Peter Schüffler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了TUM-Uteria数据集，这是一个包含216例临床病例、455对全切片图像与诊断病理报告的子宫病理数据集，经过病理学家多阶段验证，用于支持计算病理学中的全切片分析、多模态学习和自动报告生成研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏将全切片图像与临床有意义的病理报告配对的数据集，现有数据集仅关注单任务标注（如疾病分类），无法捕捉完整临床诊断工作流中的丰富信息，限制了多模态病理模型的发展。

Method: 从三级医疗中心收集了216例子宫病理临床病例，构建了455对切片级WSI-报告配对。所有数据经过由委员会认证的病理学家进行的多阶段结构化验证流程，确保注释可靠性。

Result: 构建了TUM-Uteria数据集，包含216例临床病例和455对切片级WSI-报告配对，并完成多阶段病理学家验证。

Conclusion: TUM-Uteria数据集填补了现有病理数据集中WSI与完整临床报告配对的空白，可为全切片图像分析、多模态学习和自动病理报告生成等计算病理学研究提供支撑。

Abstract: Uterine diseases represent an important category of gynecologic pathology and require accurate histopathological assessment for diagnosis and treatment planning. Whole-slide images (WSI) have enabled the digital transformation of pathology workflows and provided new opportunities for artificial intelligence (AI) in computational pathology. In particular, multimodal models that jointly analyze histopathology images and pathology reports have shown promising potential for automated pathology report generation and AI-assisted diagnosis. However, the development of such systems remains limited by the scarcity of datasets that pair whole-slide images with clinically meaningful pathology reports. Instead, existing pathology datasets focus on patch- or slide-level annotations of a single endpoint (e.g., disease class), which do not fully capture the rich information in full clinical diagnostic workflow reports. Here, we introduce TUM-Uteria, a uterine pathology dataset comprising WSIs paired with diagnostic pathology reports at both the case and slide levels, collected from a tertiary medical center. The dataset contains 216 clinical cases, comprising 455 slide-level WSI-report pairs. The dataset underwent a structured multi-stage validation procedure involving board-certified pathologists to ensure reliable annotations. TUM-Uteria supports research in computational pathology, including whole-slide image analysis, multimodal learning, and automated pathology report generation.

</details>


### [189] [SiamJEPA: On the Role of Siamese Student Encoders in JEPA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04044)
*Makoto Yamada*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SiamJEPA，在JEPA框架中使用孪生学生编码器（Siamese student encoders）进行自监督表示学习，通过实验证明该架构能有效正则化JEPA目标、提升表示可分性并加速早期训练，在有限训练预算下优于单编码器JEPA变体和MAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA方法（如I-JEPA、V-JEPA）在学生网络中仅使用单一编码器，而孪生编码器更符合脑启发表示学习框架，但其在JEPA中的作用尚未被充分探索。本文旨在研究孪生学生编码器对JEPA表示学习的影响。

Method: 提出SiamJEPA，采用掩码孪生学生编码器（masked Siamese student encoders）配合指数移动平均（EMA）教师网络，可被视为脑启发表示学习模型PhiNet的JEPA形式化。

Result: 在ImageNet线性探测实验中，SiamJEPA：1）孪生编码器作为有效正则化器，提升表示可分性并加速早期训练；2）在有限训练预算下持续优于单编码器JEPA变体；3）线性探测准确率高于需要更长训练的MAE。

Conclusion: 孪生学生编码器不仅是架构选择，更是预测性表示学习的重要归纳偏置。结合孪生学生架构为改进自监督表示学习提供了一种简单有效的方法。

Abstract: Recently, Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) have attracted significant attention in the computer vision and machine learning communities as a promising framework for self-supervised representation learning. Unlike masked autoencoders that reconstruct pixels, JEPA models learn representations by predicting latent embeddings of masked regions. Existing JEPA-based methods, such as I-JEPA and V-JEPA, typically employ a single encoder in the student network. In contrast, using Siamese encoders for student network is more naturally aligned with brain-inspired representation learning frameworks, yet their role in JEPA models remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we investigate the effect of Siamese student encoders in JEPA-based representation learning. To this end, we propose SiamJEPA, masked Siamese student encoders equipped with an exponential moving average (EMA) teacher network. SiamJEPA can also be viewed as a JEPA formulation of the brain-inspired representation learning model PhiNet. Through extensive experiments on ImageNet linear probing, we demonstrate that Siamese encoders act as an effective regularizer for the JEPA objective, improving representation separability and accelerating learning during the early stages of training. Furthermore, SiamJEPA consistently outperforms comparable single-encoder JEPA variants under limited training budgets and achieves higher linear probing accuracy than Masked Autoencoders (MAE) which requires longer training. Our findings reveal that Siamese student encoders are not merely an architectural choice but constitute an important inductive bias for predictive representation learning. These results provide new insights into the design of JEPA-based models and suggest that incorporating Siamese student architectures offers a simple yet effective approach for improving self-supervised representation learning.

</details>


### [190] [PreSIST: Vision-Language-Informed Object Persistence Prediction in Open-World Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04057)
*Amanda Adkins,Tarunvidyut Ravisankar,Joydeep Biswas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 PreSIST，通过场景上下文和视觉语言模型预测物体在任意未来时间是否仍在其最后观察位置，无需长期观测即可推理物体持久性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期感知系统只能在重访场景后被动更新地图，缺乏主动预测物体在未来是否依然存在的能力，无法在无长期观测时进行推理。

Method: PreSIST 包含两个变体：PreSIST-Lang 使用视觉语言模型（VLM）从场景上下文推断物体持久性先验；PreSIST-Vis 则使用 PreSIST-Lang 生成的伪标签训练纯视觉模型，用于高效部署。两者结合概率性持久性滤波器，随时间更新预测。

Result: 在野外物体持久性标注的新数据集上，PreSIST-Lang 和 PreSIST-Vis 在开放世界持久性预测任务上均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: PreSIST 证明了利用 VLM 的场景理解能力可在无长期观测条件下有效预测物体持久性，为机器人长期自主感知提供了主动推理的新范式。

Abstract: Robots deployed over long periods must reason about environments that change over time. Existing long-term perception systems often address object change reactively, updating their maps only after revisiting a scene and observing that an object has moved. Instead, robots should reason proactively about how long objects are likely to persist using the context in which they appear. For example, a car at a traffic light and a car in a parking spot share the same semantic class, but their contexts imply different persistence durations. We propose PreSIST (Predictive Scene-conditioned Instance Survival over Time), a method for predicting whether an observed object will remain in its last seen pose at arbitrary future times. PreSIST estimates instance-level persistence priors from object properties and scene context, then integrates these priors with a probabilistic persistence filter as observations become available. Its key insight is that the reasoning capabilities of vision-language models (VLMs) can relate scene context to likely object use and human activity, enabling persistence prediction before long-term observations are available. We develop two interchangeable variants: PreSIST-Lang, which estimates persistence priors using a VLM, and PreSIST-Vis, a novel vision-only model trained using PreSIST-Lang pseudo-labels for efficient deployment. Experiments on a new dataset of in-the-wild object persistence annotations show that PreSIST-Lang and PreSIST-Vis outperform baselines on open-world persistence prediction.

</details>


### [191] [Enhancing Implicit Neural Representations with Image Feature Embedding for Unsupervised Cardiac Cine MRI Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04069)
*Donghang Lyu,Marius Staring,Yiming Dong,Keupp Jochen,Hildo J. Lamb,Mariya Doneva*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种图像域双分支隐式神经表示框架I-FP-INR，通过增加特征处理分支提取互补特征嵌入，提升心脏电影MRI重建质量，在公共和内部数据集上均优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏电影MRI中，欠采样k-space数据需要有效的重建方法。有监督方法依赖全采样参考数据（不易获得），无监督方法中的隐式神经表示虽潜力大但原始设计能力有限，因此需要增强其表示能力以提升重建质量。

Method: 提出I-FP-INR框架，在原始隐式神经表示基础上引入额外的特征处理分支（dual-branch），通过该分支提取互补的特征嵌入，增强整体表示能力，在图像域中进行重建。

Result: 在公开数据集和内部数据上的大量评估表明，该方法在重建质量上持续优于基线方法，且在不同场景下具有强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: I-FP-INR通过双分支设计有效增强了隐式神经表示的特征提取能力，为无监督心脏电影MRI重建提供了一种高性能且鲁棒的方案。

Abstract: Cardiac cine Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) is a critical diagnostic tool that provides dynamic insights for radiologists. To accelerate acquisition, under-sampled k-space data is often used, requiring reconstruction methods that combine coil sensitivity encoding with prior information to recover missing data. Deep learning approaches have gained more attention for leveraging data-adaptive priors. While supervised learning approaches are a common choice, they depend on fully sampled reference data, which is not always available. Unsupervised methods eliminate the need for fully sampled reference data, which can be advantageous in cardiac cine MRI reconstruction. Among them, implicit neural representations (INRs) have shown great potential due to their simple architecture and good quality reconstructions. In this work, we propose an image-domain dual-branch INR framework, termed I-FP-INR, which extends the original INR design by introducing an additional feature-processing branch. This design aims to extract complementary feature embeddings to enhance the overall representation, thereby benefiting reconstruction. Extensive evaluations on both public datasets and in-house data show consistent improvements over baseline methods in reconstruction quality, with strong robustness across varied scenarios.

</details>


### [192] [Seeing Once is Enough? Online Geometry-Aware Token Pruning for 3D Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04079)
*Ruei-Chi Lai,Bolivar Solarte,Chin-Hsuan Wu,Yi-Hsuan Tsai,Min Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个无需额外训练、可在线处理的3D问答MLLM令牌剪枝方法，通过深度图和相机位姿投影到共享体素空间识别重叠区域，在Qwen2.5-VL-7B和Qwen3-VL-8B上降低50%令牌使用量并在ScanQA、SQA3D、OpenEQA-HM3D上提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在3D问答任务中需处理多视角场景，推理计算开销大；现有方法需预处理所有帧（离线方式），缺乏高效的在线处理方案。

Method: 将每帧输入通过深度信息和相机位姿投影到共享体素空间，识别跨帧的空间重叠区域，在令牌进入语言模型前选择性地剪枝冗余图像令牌，实现无需额外训练的在线处理。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B和Qwen3-VL-8B上，令牌使用量减少高达50%，在ScanQA、SQA3D、OpenEQA-HM3D基准测试中性能提升。

Conclusion: 提出的在线令牌剪枝方法能无缝集成到现有MLLM中，显著降低计算成本和内存使用，同时保持或提升3D问答任务性能。

Abstract: Recent Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable performance on 2D question answering tasks. However, extending these models to the 3D question answering remains challenging, as they typically require multiple views of the scene, which incurs substantial computational cost at inference. To mitigate this issue, existing solutions rely on strategic frame selection or token-merging algorithms that require preprocessing in advance all frames of the scene, i.e., an offline fashion. In contrast, we propose the first online token-pruning method that can be integrated seamlessly with current MLLM models for 3D question answering tasks, without additional training and with lower memory usage.Our key insight is to project each input frame into a shared voxel space using depth information and camera pose, identifying spatially-overlapped regions across frames and selectively pruning redundant image tokens before they enter the language model. Our method enables efficient online processing while reducing up to 50% of token usage. We apply this approach to Qwen2.5-VL-7B and Qwen3-VL-8B, demonstrating improved performance on the ScanQA, SQA3D, and OpenEQA-HM3D benchmarks.

</details>


### [193] [Sparse4D-Radar: An Efficient and Robust Framework for Surround-View 3D Object Detection via 4D Radar-Camera Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04098)
*Fuyuan Ai,Yuchen Tan,Jiehui Chen,Zhiwei Xu,Chunyi Song*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Sparse4D-Radar，一种基于稀疏查询的环视4D雷达-相机多模态融合框架，通过Deformable Fusion、Velocity-Consistency Sampling和Adaptive Modality Gating三个模块，在OmniHD-Scenes数据集上实现SOTA环视3D检测性能，比先前方法mAP提升超7%、ODS提升超10%，运行速度约10 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有4D雷达-相机融合方法仅支持前视感知，缺乏成熟的环视感知方案，且直接扩展到360°全覆盖会带来过高的计算成本，限制实际部署。

Method: 1) Deformable Fusion模块：将雷达-相机特征嵌入稀疏查询，构建轻量级基础版Sparse4D-Radar-Base；2) Velocity-Consistency Sampling (VCS)：利用雷达速度线索细化特征以增强运动感知；3) Adaptive Modality Gating (AMG)：根据特征置信度动态调整跨模态融合权重。组合各组件构建高精度版本Sparse4D-Radar-Acc。

Result: 在OmniHD-Scenes数据集上实现SOTA环视3D检测性能，复杂驾驶场景下mAP提升超7%、ODS提升超10%，运行速度约10 FPS，在检测精度、环境鲁棒性和推理效率间取得良好平衡。

Conclusion: Sparse4D-Radar是一种高效鲁棒的环视多模态融合框架，为4D雷达在自动驾驶环视感知中的实际部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: In recent years, 4D imaging radar has gained wide attention in autonomous driving for its robustness against harsh weather and ability to output target velocity. Nevertheless, mainstream 4D radar-camera fusion methods only support front-view perception, lacking mature solutions for surround-view sensing. Directly expanding these pipelines to full 360° coverage introduces excessive computation cost and limits real-world deployment. To tackle these limitations, this work proposes Sparse4D-Radar, an efficient robust surround-view multi-modal fusion framework. We first design a Deformable Fusion module to embed radar-camera features into sparse queries, constructing the lightweight base version Sparse4D-Radar-Base. Two dedicated modules are further introduced to boost localization accuracy and modality stability: Velocity-Consistency Sampling (VCS) refines features via radar velocity cues for motion awareness, and Adaptive Modality Gating (AMG) dynamically adjusts cross-modal fusion weights according to feature confidence. Combining all components, we build Sparse4D-Radar-Acc for high-precision detection demands. Comprehensive experiments on OmniHD-Scenes verify that our approach achieves state-of-the-art surround-view 3D detection performance. Compared with prior arts, our method obtains over 7% mAP and 10% ODS improvements under complex driving scenes while running at nearly 10 FPS, striking a favorable trade-off among detection accuracy, environmental robustness and inference efficiency. Our open-source code is available at https://github.com/Aiuan/Sparse4D-Radar.

</details>


### [194] [The Multipath Blind Spot: $K$-Agnostic Robust Calibration for Sparse-Anchor Metric Depth from Frozen Foundations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04101)
*Sohag Roy,Rajesh Misra,Swami Shastravidyananda,Tamal Maharaj*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MRAC方法，通过基于深度先验一致性的异常锚点门控机制，在无需重新训练的情况下，鲁棒地处理来自真实传感器的离群锚点（如多路径干扰、混合像素），将稀疏度量锚点与单目深度基础模型结合，实现精确的度量深度校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏锚点校准方法假设锚点数据是干净的，而真实传感器（如ToF）会产生异常值（多路径干扰、混合像素），导致传统校准方法失效。VI-Depth等方法对错误锚点存在结构性盲点。

Method: MRAC是一个无参数、推理时包装器，通过Theil-Sen拟合和中位数绝对偏差检验，基于深度先验的一致性来筛选锚点，然后仅用筛选后的锚点调用一次校准头。

Result: 在320个基准测试中，MRAC在84%的同骨干网络单元上获胜；在KITTI多路径场景中，AbsRel降低3.2倍（从0.489降至0.151），无需重新训练。

Conclusion: MRAC通过无参数、推理时的一致性筛选机制，有效解决了真实传感器异常锚点对单目深度校准的干扰问题，显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Monocular depth foundations predict domain-general relative depth but lack absolute scale; a handful of sparse metric anchors from a range sensor can calibrate them to metric depth, an attractive alternative to metric-supervised training. Existing sparse-anchor calibration methods, however, assume the anchors are clean, whereas real sensors produce outliers that are present with the wrong value -- time-of-flight multipath, mixed pixels -- not merely missing. We show that the established residual-on-CFA calibration recipe collapses under such outliers, and that the strongest publicly deployed method, VI-Depth, has a structural multipath blind spot: robust to missing anchors, it falls behind an unprotected baseline on three of four datasets when anchors are present but wrong. We propose Multipath-Robust Anchor Calibration (MRAC), a parameter-free, inference-time wrapper that gates anchors by foundation consistency -- a Theil--Sen fit and a median-absolute-deviation test against the foundation's own relative-depth ordering -- before a single call to the calibration head. MRAC adds no learned parameters, runs its selection in $\approx 50\,μ$s on CPU, and serves anchor budgets $K \in [5,200]$ from one checkpoint. On a $320$-cell benchmark with a same-backbone, same-architecture control, MRAC strictly wins $84\%$ of same-backbone cells across all four outlier families and, against VI-Depth, wins all twelve corrupted multipath cells and all sixteen KITTI cells, reducing KITTI multipath AbsRel by $3.2\times$ ($0.489$ to $0.151$) at zero retraining.

</details>


### [195] [SOV-CAD: Stepwise Orthographic Views Guided CAD Modeling Sequence Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04119)
*Zhaopeng Feng,Chen Zhi,Xuhong Zhang,Zhengwen Feng,Xinkui Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SOV-CAD框架，利用逐步视觉监督和离线强化学习（Decision Transformer）从图像重建CAD建模序列，超越了现有方法并具有良好的数据效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CAD重建方法通常整体生成完整序列，忽略了人类设计流程中迭代、反馈驱动的特性。作者希望通过引入逐步视觉监督（每一步观察目标正交投影、当前模型投影和活动草图）来弥补这一局限。

Method: 将CAD重建建模为序贯决策任务，使用离线强化学习和Decision Transformer架构，并结合连续的几何对齐奖励视觉反馈来指导动作选择。

Result: 在CAD序列重建任务上超越了现有最优方法，并展现出强大的数据效率。

Conclusion: SOV-CAD通过将逐步视觉反馈融入决策式重建，实现了更准确、更符合人类建模习惯的CAD序列重建，且数据效率更高。

Abstract: Reconstructing Computer-Aided Design (CAD) modeling sequences from images is crucial for preserving design intent and supporting parametric editing. However, existing methods typically generate full CAD sequences holistically, overlooking the iterative, feedback-driven nature of human design workflows. We address this limitation by introducing the rich stepwise visual supervision: at each modeling step, the system observes the target's orthographic projections, the projections of the incrementally constructed model, and the active sketch, enabling informed action selection. To effectively leverage this on-the-fly feedback, we propose SOV-CAD, a framework that formulates CAD reconstruction as a sequential decision-making task and employs offline reinforcement learning with a Decision Transformer architecture. This design incorporates continuous visual feedback guided by geometric alignment rewards, resulting in a more accurate and human-like modeling process. Extensive experiments show that SOV-CAD surpasses state-of-the-art methods in CAD sequence reconstruction while exhibiting strong data efficiency. Code of SOV-CAD is available at: https://github.com/LukePhong/SOV-CAD

</details>


### [196] [FRFDet: Efficient UAV Small Object Detection with Symmetric Sampling and Scalable Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04125)
*Yunzhong Si,Huiying Xu,Xinzhong Zhu,Yang Liu,Yao Dong,Wenhao Zhang,Hongbo Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FRFDet，一种轻量级单阶段无人机小目标检测器，通过逆双向采样（IBS）和尺度特征关系交叉融合（SFRCF）两个即插即用模块，在复杂环境（恶劣天气、低光照、传感器噪声）下实现高效小目标检测，在VisDrone、UAVDT、HazyDet和MS COCO上达到轻量级检测器SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机图像小目标检测在复杂天气、低光照和传感器噪声等恶劣条件下仍面临挑战，主要源于严重背景杂波、细粒度细节退化以及次优的语义-空间特征融合，这些问题共同阻碍了鲁棒的小目标表示。

Method: 提出FRFDet轻量级单阶段检测器，包含两个即插即用模块：1）逆双向采样（IBS），通过通道扩展-压缩和双向模式重建保留关键空间细节，改善特征对齐；2）尺度特征关系交叉融合（SFRCF），显式建模尺度相关的融合行为，发现组间逐元素乘法有利于紧凑模型，而组间加性融合更适合大型架构。

Result: 在VisDrone、UAVDT、HazyDet和MS COCO数据集上，FRFDet在轻量级检测器中实现了最先进的性能，具有低计算成本、紧凑参数和快速推理速度。

Conclusion: FRFDet适用于资源受限的无人机平台，在恶劣条件下仍能保持高效的小目标检测能力。

Abstract: Small object detection in Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) imagery remains challenging under adverse conditions, including complex weather, low illumination, and sensor noise. These challenges mainly stem from severe background clutter, fine-grained detail degradation, and suboptimal semantic-spatial feature fusion, which jointly hinder robust small-object representation. To this end, we propose FRFDet, a lightweight yet effective single-stage detector tailored for UAV-based small object detection. FRFDet proposes two plug-and-play modules: Inverse Bidirectional Sampling (IBS) and Scale-Feature Relationship Cross-Fusion (SFRCF). IBS preserves critical spatial details via channel expansion-compression and bidirectional pattern reconstruction, improving feature alignment. SFRCF explicitly models scale-dependent fusion behaviors, revealing that inter-group element-wise multiplication favors compact models, while inter-group additive fusion benefits larger architectures. Extensive experiments on VisDrone, UAVDT, HazyDet, and MS COCO demonstrate that FRFDet achieves state-of-the-art performance among lightweight detectors with low computational cost, compact parameters, and fast inference, making it well suited for resource-constrained UAV platforms.

</details>


### [197] [Real-Time LiDAR Gaussian Splatting SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04127)
*Seungjun Tak,Yewon Jeon,Jaeik Hwang,SukMin Hwang,Seongbo Ha,Hyeonwoo Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于LiDAR的实时高斯泼溅SLAM框架，通过紧密耦合快速G-ICP配准与球形光栅化稠密建图，在大规模序列上实现实时（>20 FPS）高精度建图与定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LiDAR SLAM方法在建图精度、实时性和大规模场景可扩展性之间难以兼顾，缺乏高效利用LiDAR几何信息进行稠密建图与跟踪联合优化的框架。

Method: 1) 快速G-ICP配准与球形光栅化稠密建图紧耦合；2) 复用跟踪估计的局部协方差初始化高斯体，赋予距离感知尺度并推导表面法向量用于几何感知优化；3) 引入协方差导出的几何分数，驱动平面区域剪枝和结构丰富区选择性密化；4) 优化的高斯体与LiDAR置信度反馈提升跟踪鲁棒性。

Result: 在Newer College数据集上，纯在线轨迹下F-score达86.78%，帧率>20 FPS；在其他数据集上验证了方法的稳定性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: 所提框架在兼顾实时性的同时显著提升了稠密建图精度，且具备良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: We present a real-time LiDAR-based framework for Gaussian Splatting SLAM that tightly couples fast G-ICP registration with spherical rasterization-based dense mapping for large-scale sequences. Leveraging LiDAR geometry rather than appearance, we reuse tracking-estimated local covariances to initialize Gaussians with range-aware scales and to derive surface normals for geometry-aware map optimization. We further introduce a covariance-derived geometry score that measures local complexity and drives pruning in planar regions and selective densification in structurally rich areas, while optimized Gaussians and LiDAR-specific confidence cues are fed back to improve tracking robustness. On the Newer College dataset, our method achieves an F-score of 86.78\% using purely online trajectories at real-time speed ($>$20 FPS), and additional experiments on other datasets confirm its stability and scalability.

</details>


### [198] [HCSU: A Dataset and Benchmark for Fine-Grained Historical Calligraphy Style Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04147)
*Yinsheng Yao,Yan Liu,Chen Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了HCSU——首个面向历史书法风格细粒度理解的综合数据集，包含39,307张来自49位书法家、跨越10个朝代的字符图像，通过区分墨迹（帖）与石刻（碑）解决模态混合问题，并提供分层专家美学描述以支持细粒度风格判别和可解释美学推理评估。实验表明当前LVLM在书法风格感知上仍存在显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型对书法风格的细粒度感知能力不足，主要受限于现有数据集的模态混合（墨迹与石刻混杂）和扁平化标签问题，亟需构建高质量、结构化、具有专家知识支撑的评测基准。

Method: 构建HCSU数据集：收集39,307张来自49位历史著名书法家、涵盖10个朝代的字符图像；系统地将真迹墨迹（帖）与石刻拓本（碑）分离以消除模态混合；提供层级式专家撰写的审美描述；设计两种评估协议：细粒度风格判别和可解释美学推理。

Result: 当前最先进的LVLM在书法风格感知上表现有限；模型对字体类型、文本内容和来源线索敏感；难以将审美判断与细粒度笔法证据相结合；存在书法知识与视觉风格感知之间的显著差距。

Conclusion: HCSU基准揭示了当前多模态架构在文化理解方面的根本性局限，旨在推动面向文化遗产保护的专家级视觉推理能力发展。

Abstract: Automated fine-grained perception of calligraphy styles--a task vital to cultural heritage preservation--remains a critical challenge for Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs), largely constrained by existing datasets that suffer from modal mixture and flattened labels. To bridge this gap, we introduce HCSU, the first comprehensive dataset tailored for fine-grained Historical Calligraphy Style Understanding. HCSU comprises 39,307 meticulously curated character images from 49 historically prominent calligraphers across 10 dynasties, systematically decoupling authentic ink manuscripts (Tie) from stone rubbings (Bei) to resolve the long-standing modal mixture problem. Moving beyond conventional flattened labels, HCSU provides hierarchical expert-written aesthetic descriptions, enabling two rigorous evaluation protocols: fine-grained style discrimination and interpretable aesthetic reasoning. Extensive evaluations reveal a persistent gap between calligraphy-related knowledge and visually grounded style perception: state-of-the-art LVLMs show non-trivial performance but remain sensitive to script-level, textual, and source-specific cues, and often struggle to ground aesthetic judgments in fine-grained brushwork evidence. Ultimately, the HCSU benchmark exposes fundamental limitations in current multimodal architectures, aiming to inspire the evolution of expert-level visual reasoning for cultural heritage preservation. The dataset is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Tongji209/HCSU.

</details>


### [199] [Beyond Scene Priors: Fine-Grained Traffic Scene Reasoning with Benchmarking and Query-Guided Small-Object Focus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04149)
*Waikit Xiu,Qiang Lu,Zian Wang,Xinjie Yang,Zhiwei Chen,Chen Sun,Xiying Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对交通场景中多模态大语言模型（MLLM）对关键小目标关注不足的问题（称为“关键证据稀释”），提出了细粒度交通推理基准FGTR-Bench（40,236道多选题+4,947盲测集）以及文本引导的小目标推理MLLM——TSR-MLLM。TSR-MLLM基于Qwen3-VL-4B，通过查询条件化的Text-Guided Small-Object Focus（TG-SOF）图，在解码器边界对最相关的视觉槽施加稀疏Top-K门控残差，无需外部检测器或图像重新编码即可保持单次推理。在匹配设置下，TSR-MLLM在FGTR-Bench上以74.1%的整体准确率超越最强4B基线2.1个百分点，在证据定位轨道上增益更大，同时在DriveQA-V（CARLA Signs）上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MLLM在安全关键的交通场景中倾向于过度关注背景，压倒性的大型背景会稀释关键小目标的视觉-语言对齐信号，导致“关键证据稀释”的失败模式。同时现有VQA数据集缺乏大规模、高干扰项的评估来暴露这一问题。

Method: 1) 构建FGTR-Bench：通过多智能体生成、一致性检查和专家审核创建40,236道单选题及4,947盲测集。2) 提出TSR-MLLM：基于Qwen3-VL-4B，引入查询条件化的Text-Guided Small-Object Focus (TG-SOF)图，在解码器边界对最相关的视觉槽施加稀疏Top-K门控残差，同时保持文本token不变，结合轻量级解码器适配，实现单次推理且无需外部检测器或图像重编码。

Result: 在匹配设置下，TSR-MLLM在FGTR-Bench上以74.1%整体准确率超越最强4B基线2.1个百分点，在证据定位轨道上增益更大。在DriveQA-V（CARLA Signs）上无需任务特定微调，贪心解码下仍保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 所提出的基准和模型有效填补了交通场景细粒度推理的评估空白和方法空白，通过文本引导的稀疏注意力机制解决了关键证据稀释问题，在保持推理效率的同时显著提升了小目标相关问答的准确性。

Abstract: In safety-critical traffic scenarios, answering complex questions relies on minute, localized visual cues. However, standard Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) tend to over-attend to backgrounds, overwhelming crucial small objects during visual-language alignment, a failure mode we term 'critical evidence dilution.' Furthermore, existing visual question answering (VQA) datasets rarely expose this flaw, as they lack large-scale, distractor-heavy evaluations that require pinpointing local evidence. To bridge this evaluation and architecture gap, we introduce the Fine-Grained Traffic Reasoning Benchmark (FGTR-Bench) and the Text-Guided Small-Object Reasoning MLLM (TSR-MLLM). FGTR-Bench comprises 40,236 single-image Multiple-Choice Questions (MCQs) created via multi-agent generation, consistency checks, and expert audits, alongside a disjoint 4,947-sample blind test split. To resolve evidence dilution, TSR-MLLM, built on Qwen3-VL-4B, uses a query-conditioned Text-Guided Small-Object Focus (TG-SOF) map. Applied once at the decoder boundary, the map adds sparse Top-K gated residuals to the most question-relevant vision slots while leaving text tokens unchanged. Together with lightweight decoder adaptation, TSR-MLLM preserves single-pass inference without external detectors or image re-encoding. Under matched settings, TSR-MLLM outperforms the strongest 4B baseline by 2.1 points on FGTR-Bench (74.1% overall), with larger gains on evidence-local tracks. Furthermore, it remains competitive on DriveQA-V (CARLA Signs) under greedy decoding without task-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [200] [Perceiving Better Moments: Cover Frame Reselection and Enhancement for Live Photos with the Live2K Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04151)
*Junyu Lou,Kai Chen,Weiyi You,Hui Zeng,Lei Zhang,Shuhang Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文定义了Live Photo封面帧重选与增强（LPRE）任务，构建了包含2,042张Live Photo的真实数据集Live2K，并提出了一个集多帧融合、引导色彩增强和超分辨率于一体的统一单阶段基线模型，为Live Photo增强研究建立了首个基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能手机的Live Photo中，封面照片与视频帧因采用不同的成像管线（照片经完整计算摄影处理，视频受实时性和压缩限制），存在显著的质量差距（分辨率、色彩保真度、动态范围）。当用户从视频中重选帧替换不理想的封面时，所选帧质量严重退化，直接替换视觉效果不佳。现有超分辨率或色彩增强方法难以同时处理空间细节和色彩外观的联合恢复。

Method: 定义LPRE任务，利用Live Photo内的高质量封面图像作为结构和色彩参考、用户重选的低质量帧作为重建目标、相邻视频帧提供时序线索；构建包含2,042张Live Photo的真实世界数据集Live2K；提出统一的单阶段基线模型，集成多帧融合、引导色彩增强和超分辨率。

Result: 构建了Live2K数据集并建立了首个Live Photo增强基准。

Conclusion: 所提出的LPRE任务和基准模型填补了Live Photo封面帧重选与增强领域的空白，为后续研究提供了标准化评估平台。

Abstract: Modern smartphones capture Live Photos, short video bursts surrounding a still image, offering a dynamic and engaging photographic experience. However, the cover photo and video components are generated by two distinct imaging pipelines: the photo stream undergoes full computational photography processing, while the video stream is constrained by real-time efficiency and heavy compression. This intrinsic separation produces a substantial quality gap in resolution, color fidelity, and dynamic range between the cover photo and video frames. When users reselect an alternative frame from the video to replace an imperfect cover, the chosen frame often suffers from severe degradation, making direct replacement visually unsatisfactory. Restoring such frames requires simultaneous enhancement of spatial detail and color appearance, a task considerably more challenging than ordinary super-resolution or color enhancement. To address this, we define the Live Photo Cover Frame Reselection and Enhancement (LPRE) task, which leverages the intrinsic cues available within each Live Photo: the high-quality cover image as a structural and color reference, the user-reselected low-quality frame as the reconstruction target and several adjacent video frames providing temporal cues. Building upon this formulation, we construct Live2K, a real-world dataset of 2,042 Live Photos, and develop a unified one-stage baseline that integrates multi-frame fusion, guided color enhancement and super-resolution, establishing the first benchmark for Live Photo enhancement research.

</details>


### [201] [SeeMe: Mitigating Hallucinations in Large Vision-Language Models through Effective Visual Token Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04163)
*Kai Tang,Jinhao You,Bohua Zhang,Yichen Guo,Yiding Sun,Dongxu Zhang,Chenxi Li,Xiande Huang,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SeeMe提出了一种无需训练的特征工程框架，通过对视觉令牌进行三阶段重构，抑制噪声令牌以减少LVLM中的幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM幻觉缓解方法主要干预解码阶段，忽视了无关或噪声视觉令牌误导解码过程这一关键根源。

Method: 提出SeeMe，一个无需训练的框架，通过三阶段令牌工程（token engineering）重构视觉令牌，抑制幻觉源同时保留信息性视觉证据。

Result: 在MME、POPE、AMBER基准测试及四个LVLM上的实验表明，SeeMe持续减少幻觉并提高输出一致性。

Conclusion: SeeMe为缓解LVLM中的幻觉提供了从特征工程视角出发的新途径。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have achieved remarkable progress in visual understanding tasks such as image captioning and visual question answering. However, they remain susceptible to hallucinations, generating content that is inconsistent with the actual visual input. Existing methods primarily intervene at the decoding stage, while overlooking a critical source of hallucinations: irrelevant or noisy visual tokens that mislead the decoding process. To address this issue, we propose SeeMe, a training-free framework that introduces the concept of feature engineering from traditional machine learning into LVLMs. SeeMe restructures visual tokens through a three-stage token engineering process to suppress hallucination sources while preserving informative visual evidence. Experiments on MME, POPE, and AMBER benchmarks across four LVLMs demonstrate that SeeMe consistently reduces hallucinations and improves output consistency, providing a novel perspective for mitigating hallucinations in LVLMs.

</details>


### [202] [CritiqueDriveVLM: From Verifier-Guided Reinforcement Learning to Latent Thought Distillation for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04179)
*Zhaohong Liu,Hao Ye,Xianlin Zhang,Mengshi Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CritiqueDriveVLM提出了一种三阶段框架，通过批判驱动的多轮强化学习（提升MCQ准确率从55.54%到76.54%）和潜在思维蒸馏（将推理压缩至平均28个token，延迟降低88%至416ms），在自动驾驶中解决了VLM推理可靠性与效率之间的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端VLM在自动驾驶中潜力巨大，但标准SFT存在推理幻觉和保守偏差；传统工具增强框架和CoT方法虽然能缓解这些问题，但token消耗过大、延迟不可接受，无法实现实时部署。因此需要解决可靠性-效率权衡问题。

Method: 提出统一的三阶段框架：1) 批判驱动的多轮强化学习，通过多维验证器提供粒度的标量反馈和多轮惩罚，促使策略内化逻辑推理，训练出无需外部工具的System-2 Teacher；2) 潜在思维蒸馏，将Teacher的收敛推理状态与Student的潜在表示对齐，压缩深度逻辑能力到快速、无CoT的System-1 Student。

Result: 在DriveLMM-01基准上，MCQ从55.54%提升至76.54%（SOTA）；蒸馏后的Student推理长度平均仅28个token，推理延迟从3482ms降至416ms（降低88%）。

Conclusion: CritiqueDriveVLM成功实现了自动驾驶中VLM的高可靠性与低延迟的统一，为实时部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: End-to-end Vision-Language Models (VLMs) show immense potential in autonomous driving. However, standard Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) often suffers from reasoning hallucinations and conservative biases. While traditional tool-augmented frameworks and Chain-of-Thought (CoT) approaches mitigate these issues, they incur exorbitant token consumption and unacceptable latency, rendering real-time deployment impractical. To resolve this reliability-efficiency trade-off, we propose CritiqueDriveVLM, a novel unified three-stage framework internalizing reasoning directly into the VLM. First, we introduce Critique-Driven Multi-Turn Reinforcement Learning (RL) guided by a multi-dimensional verifier. By providing granular scalar feedback and a multi-turn penalty, we force the policy to internalize logical deduction, cultivating a robust System-2 Teacher that achieves high accuracy without fragile external tools. Subsequently, we propose Latent Thought Distillation to overcome the latency bottleneck. By aligning the Student's latent representations with the Teacher's fully converged reasoning states, we compress deep logical capabilities into a fast, CoT-free System-1 Student. Extensive experiments on the widely-used DriveLMM-01 benchmark demonstrate remarkable improvements. Compared to the base model, our tool-free Teacher significantly boosts Multiple Choice Quality (MCQ) from 55.54% to a state-of-the-art 76.54%. Crucially, our distilled Student preserves competitive reasoning depth while drastically minimizing generation length to an average of merely 28 tokens. This slashes inference latency by 88% (from 3482 ms to 416 ms), paving a highly robust pathway for low-latency autonomous driving.Our source code is available at https://github.com/MICLAB-BUPT/CritiqueDriveVLM.

</details>


### [203] [Topology-Driven Transferability Estimation for 3D Medical Vision Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04199)
*Jiaqi Tang,Shaoyang Zhang,Fandong Zhang,Shu Zhang,Yang Liu,Qingchao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需微调、基于拓扑学的非参数化迁移性评估框架，通过最小生成树构建密集特征的稀疏1-骨架图，并解耦为局部边界感知拓扑一致性和全局表征拓扑散度两个互补尺度，在114,000个3D医学体积上实现SOTA评估性能，加速56倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有迁移性评估方法依赖穷举微调（计算昂贵），且多为图像级分类设计，无法保留分割任务所需的细粒度边界信息与空间关系；3D医学影像的密集预测需要体素级评估，现有指标不适用。

Method: 提出非参数化的拓扑驱动框架：1) 通过最小生成树(MST)构建密集特征的稀疏1-骨架图；2) 解耦为两个几何尺度——局部边界感知拓扑一致性(LBTC，证明MST泄漏率是贝叶斯误差的有限样本下界)和全局表征拓扑散度(GRTD)；3) 利用随机初始化解码器作为拓扑保持空间投影器；4) 通过任务自适应门控机制融合双指标。

Result: 在114,000个3D医学体积的大规模基准上，平均加权Kendall系数提升0.36，评估速度加快56倍，达到SOTA迁移性估计性能。

Conclusion: 拓扑驱动框架有效解决了医学分割任务中无需微调的迁移性评估难题，证明了随机初始化解码器的拓扑保持特性，为大规模医学基础模型选择提供了高效方案。

Abstract: The growing number of medical vision foundation models highlights the need for effective model selection. However, mainstream selection methods rely on exhaustive fine-tuning, which is computationally expensive. Most of the existing Transferability Estimation (TE) metrics are primarily designed for image-level classification. They fail to preserve spatial relationships and fine-grained boundary details, which are crucial for the segmentation task. Additionally, while image-level tasks typically process a single feature vector per input, dense prediction tasks in 3D medical imaging require voxel-wise evaluation against dense annotations. To bridge these gaps, we propose a \textit{non-parametric, topology-driven} framework that estimates transferability directly from the alignment between the sparse 1-skeleton graph of dense features and semantic labels via Minimum Spanning Trees (MST). We decouple the alignment into two complementary geometric scales: Local Boundary-Aware Topological Consistency (LBTC) to assess boundary separability, where we prove that the MST leakage rate serves as a finite-sample lower bound on the Bayes error; and Global Representation Topology Divergence (GRTD) to evaluate the overall anatomical layout. Crucially, we formally justify a counterintuitive mechanism: Although without fine-tuning, the randomly initialized segmentation decoder acts as a topology-preserving spatial projector, reducing the variance of pairwise distance estimates and stabilizing global alignment evaluation. Fused via a task-adaptive gating mechanism, these dual metrics adapt to diverse clinical complexities. Evaluated on a large-scale benchmark of 114,000 3D medical volumes across diverse anatomical tasks, our topological framework achieves state-of-the-art transferability estimation with an average weighted Kendall (outperforming by 0.36) while accelerating evaluation by 56 times.

</details>


### [204] [Hierarchical Multi-to-Single-Modal Knowledge Distillation for Disruption Prediction in EAST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04241)
*Qiang Chen,Xiao Wang,Hao Si,Qingquan Yang,Meiwen Chen,Jianhua Yang,Xiaofeng Han,Yunhu Jia,Ran Chen,Liang Wang,Jin Tang,Guosheng Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出层次化多到单模态知识蒸馏框架，在训练时利用可见图像和时间序列信号训练多模态教师模型，推理时仅保留时间序列学生模型，在EAST数据集上验证了该方法在保持多模态学习判别优势的同时大幅降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等离子体破裂预测中，可见图像模态能提供空间线索（等离子体形变、局部亮化、辐射结构演化），显著提升模型判别能力，但图像模态大幅增加推理计算成本。为兼顾多模态优势与推理效率，提出层次化多到单模态知识蒸馏框架。

Method: 提出层次化多到单模态知识蒸馏框架：训练阶段，使用可见图像和时间序列信号通过Transformer编码器和原型引导时空超图模块训练多模态教师模型；推理阶段，仅保留时间序列学生模型，通过图结构级、表示级和决策级三级蒸馏传递多模态知识。

Result: 在EAST 640次放电数据集上，该框架能够保留多模态学习的判别优势，同时显著降低推理成本。

Conclusion: 该方法为EAST装置提供了一种高效的破裂预测路径，兼顾了多模态信息的判别能力与单模态推理的计算效率。

Abstract: Plasma disruption is a critical threat to tokamak safety. Existing data-driven predictors mainly rely on time-series diagnostic signals, while visible images provide complementary spatial cues including plasma deformation, local brightening, and radiation-structure evolution. Although the image modality improves the model's discriminative capability, it also substantially increases the computational cost during inference. To address this issue, we propose a hierarchical multi-to-single-modal knowledge distillation framework for disruption prediction on a synchronized EAST multimodal dataset. During training, visible images and time-series signals are used to train a multimodal teacher, which learns disruption precursor representations through Transformer-based encoders and a prototype-guided spatiotemporal hypergraph module. During inference, only the time-series student is retained, with multimodal knowledge transferred through graph-structure-level, representation-level, and decision-level distillation. On the 640-discharge EAST dataset, the results demonstrate that the proposed framework can preserve the discriminative advantages of multimodal learning while substantially reducing inference cost, and providing an effective route for efficient disruption prediction in EAST. The source code of this paper will be released on https://github.com/Event-AHU/OpenFusion.

</details>


### [205] [HeartVolMesh: Cardiac Volumetric Mesh Reconstruction via Covariance-Guided Graph Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04243)
*Fengming Lin,Arezoo Zakeri,Haoran Dou,Zherui Zhou,Shaokun Lan,Jinming Duan,Alejandro Frangi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HeartVolMesh，利用3D CNN-GNN从体积图像预测顶点位移和协方差，并通过变形四面体模板生成患者特异性心脏网格，在保持拓扑对应关系的同时提升薄壁结构精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分割-建模流程会模糊薄壁解剖结构，且跨病例对应关系有限，无法满足高精度患者特异性四面体心脏网格的生成需求。

Method: 将每个模板顶点提升为各向异性高斯核，使用3D CNN-GNN预测顶点位移和Cholesky参数化的协方差；通过协方差感知的负对数似然损失和轻量网格正则化训练；采用分阶段对齐、非刚性配准和变形传播将固定四面体模板变形到重建表面。

Result: 在表面网格精度和体积网格保真度上，持续优于基于变形的基线方法。

Conclusion: HeartVolMesh能有效生成具有拓扑对应关系的高保真患者特异性四面体心脏网格，适用于计算机模拟试验。

Abstract: Accurate patient-specific tetrahedral cardiac meshes are essential for in-silico trials, yet common segmentation-then-modelling pipelines can blur thin-wall anatomy and offer limited cross-case correspondence. We propose HeartVolMesh, which lifts each template vertex to an anisotropic Gaussian kernel and uses a 3D CNN-GNN to predict per-vertex displacements and Cholesky-parameterized covariances from volumetric images. Training is guided by a covariance-aware negative log-likelihood loss with lightweight mesh regularization. For volumetric meshing, we warp a fixed tetrahedral template to the reconstructed surface via staged alignment, non-rigid registration, and deformation propagation, preserving connectivity and correspondence by construction, with resolution controlled by template density. Experiments show consistent gains over deformation-based baselines in surface mesh accuracy and volumetric mesh fidelity.

</details>


### [206] [Beyond Random Sampling: Distribution-Aware Alignment for Semi-Supervised Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04249)
*Weihao Yan,Yeqiang Qian,Yi Dong,Ming Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于分布对齐的高数据效率半监督医学图像分割框架，通过分布感知样本选择（利用VFM和密度K中心算法）和记忆引导复制粘贴模块（MCP），在极低标注比例下实现强分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统半监督医学图像分割假设数据独立同分布，默认随机采样，在低数据场景下存在严重的表示偏差，无法捕获异质医学数据流形，导致数据利用效率低下。

Method: 1) 离线分布感知样本选择：利用视觉基础模型(VFM)和设计的密度K中心算法识别代表性结构锚点，构建更具代表性的标注域；2) 记忆引导复制粘贴(MCP)模块：针对医学扫描固有的类别不平衡问题，利用语义记忆机制检索历史一致先验进行跨域对齐，促进语义一致性；3) 配合易到难的渐进式调度，缓解早期伪标签噪声。

Result: 在六个多样化的2D和3D数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在极低标注比例（如1/16）下实现了强大的分割性能。

Conclusion: 通过分布对齐策略显著提升了半监督医学图像分割在低数据场景下的数据效率和分割精度，有效解决了表示偏差问题。

Abstract: Precise medical image segmentation is crucial for clinical diagnosis and treatment planning, yet relies heavily on expensive expert annotations. Semi-supervised medical image segmentation (SSMIS) offers a cost-effective solution but typically operates under the assumption of independent and identically distributed (i.i.d.) data, defaulting to random sampling. While statistically valid at scale, this strategy suffers from severe representation bias in low-data regimes, failing to capture the heterogeneous medical data manifold. To address this, we propose a highly data-efficient framework driven by distribution alignment. First, we introduce an offline Distribution-Aware Sample Selection strategy. By leveraging Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) and our designed Density-K-Center algorithm, we explicitly identify representative structural anchors, establishing a more representative labeled domain. Second, to bridge the remaining distribution gap, we propose the Memory-guided Copy-Paste (MCP) module. Tailored for the inherent class imbalance in medical scans, MCP leverages a semantic memory mechanism to retrieve historically consistent priors for cross-domain alignment, encouraging semantic consistency. Coupled with an easy-to-hard progressive schedule, this framework effectively mitigates early-stage pseudo-label noise. Extensive experiments on six diverse 2D and 3D datasets demonstrate strong segmentation performance, particularly in extremely low-labeled scenarios (\eg, 1/16 ratio).

</details>


### [207] [AdaptiveSplat:Texture Aware Controllable 3D Gaussian Allocation for Feed-Forward Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04256)
*Badrinath Singhal,Srihari K G,Sreehari Iyer,Ankit Dhiman,Venkatesh Babu Radhakrishnan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用局部纹理信息显式控制高斯原语数量的前馈式3D重建方法，通过纹理估计、纹理感知剪枝和自适应高斯头三个关键组件，在保持前馈范式的同时去除低频区域的冗余高斯原语，实验证明该方法在多个数据集上有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈式3D重建方法预测像素对齐的高斯原语，产生高度冗余的表示。虽然自然想到剪枝冗余高斯，但朴素剪枝会引入严重伪影且通常需要推理时微调，破坏了前馈范式。高频区域需要更多高斯原语，而低频区域可用更少原语表示，这启发了本文工作。

Method: (1) 纹理估计：捕捉场景细节的空间变化；(2) 纹理感知剪枝：从低频区域移除冗余高斯原语；(3) 自适应高斯头：预测保留原语的修改后属性，且不破坏前馈范式。

Result: 在RE10K、ACID、DL3DV、Tanks and Temples、DTU等多个数据集上的实验验证了方法的有效性，消融研究证实了各关键组件的贡献。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于局部纹理信息显式控制高斯原语数量的前馈式3D重建方法，通过纹理感知剪枝有效减少了冗余而不引入伪影，且无需推理时微调，保持了前馈范式的优势。

Abstract: Current feed-forward 3D reconstruction methods predict pixel aligned Gaussian primitives, resulting in highly redundant representations. A natural solution is to prune the redundant Gaussians, but naive pruning introduces severe artifacts and often requires inference time fine-tuning, breaking the feed-forward paradigm. Based on previous works, high frequency regions require more Gaussian primitives, while low frequency regions can be represented with significantly fewer primitives. Motivated by this, we propose a novel approach to explicitly control the number of Gaussians by leveraging local texture information. Our approach achieves this through three key components: (1) texture estimation to capture spatial variation in scene detail, (2) texture-aware pruning that removes redundant Gaussians from low frequency regions, and (3) an adaptive Gaussian head that predicts the modified attributes of the retained primitives without breaking the feed-forward paradigm. Experiments on RE10K, ACID, DL3DV, Tanks and Temples, and DTU demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, while ablation studies validate the contributions of its key components.

</details>


### [208] [EMPURPLE: A Free Lunch for Diffusion Distillation based on the Information Bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04276)
*Zilai Li,Lujia Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 扩散蒸馏模型在推理时高效但FID退化，本文通过PAC泛化界分析发现早期速度场重定向导致蒸馏目标难以学习、训练-测试差距扩大。提出无需训练的EMPURPLE方法，通过复用原始模型的中间潜变量，在多个蒸馏模型上将FID提升7%-20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型推理成本高，蒸馏虽能减少采样步数，但许多蒸馏模型（如SDXL-Lightning、DMD等）面临FID退化问题，缺乏对这一现象的深入理论分析。

Method: 1. 通过PAC风格泛化界理论分析蒸馏模型FID退化机制，指出早期速度场重定向导致训练-测试分布不匹配；2. 提出EMPURPLE，一种无需训练的推理方法，通过复用原始扩散模型的中间潜变量来增强生成多样性。

Result: EMPURPLE在DMD2、Hyper-SD、FlashSD、SDXL-Lightning等多个蒸馏模型上将FID提升7%-20%，且模型无关、无需额外训练。

Conclusion: 蒸馏模型FID退化的根源在于早期速度场重定向导致的分布不匹配，EMPURPLE通过简单有效的训练后处理方法显著改善生成质量与多样性。

Abstract: Diffusion models achieve impressive image-generation quality but remain expensive at inference time. Diffusion distillation reduces sampling steps, yet many distilled models, including SDXL-Lightning and distribution matching distillation methods, suffer from degraded Fréchet Inception Distance (FID). We analyze this phenomenon through a PAC-style generalization bound. Our analysis suggests that aggressive early-step redirection of the velocity field makes the distillation target harder to learn, enlarging the train-test gap. As a result, early-step output distributions differ between training and inference, causing distribution mismatch in the intermediate noisy latent used as next-step inputs. We empirically validate this mechanism by showing reduced diversity in both intermediate features and final outputs. To address this issue, we propose EMPURPLE, a simple training-free method that recycles intermediate latents sampled from the original model. EMPURPLE is model-agnostic and improves FID by 7\% to 20\% across DMD2, Hyper-SD, FlashSD, and SDXL-Lightning. The repo is: https://github.com/TheLovesOfLadyPurple/Empurple-Training-Free-Algorithm-To-enhance-Diversity-of-The-Diffusion-Distillation-Model

</details>


### [209] [AquaStereo: Enabling Underwater Stereo Matching via Depth-Conditioned Diffusion and Geometry Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04303)
*Qizhe Wei,Yingping Liang,Shaodi You,Ying Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: AquaStereo 提出了一种面向水下立体匹配的感知增强框架，包含数据模拟流水线（深度条件扩散生成水下立体对+左右一致性几何对齐）和自蒸馏策略（冻结的陆地双目教师指导学生网络），并引入可学习感知帧（融合时序与语义特征构建鲁棒匹配描述子），显著改善了水下场景的鲁棒性和零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习的立体匹配模型在水下环境中面临两大挑战：水下领域内训练数据稀缺，以及水下图像退化（浑浊、低纹理等）导致难以提取判别性特征进行匹配。

Method: (1) 深度条件扩散流水线生成水下立体对，联合轻量级左右一致性模块保证几何对齐；(2) 自蒸馏策略：冻结的陆地图像训练的双目教师指导学生网络（接收含扰动的水下立体对），采用阶段加权序列损失对齐视差，并通过干净分支监督与共享伪目标防止尺度漂移；(3) 可学习感知帧：利用视频 backbone 编码两个辅助视图的时序线索，与强图像编码器提取的语义特征融合，构建鲁棒匹配描述子。

Result: 大量实验表明 AquaStereo 在挑战性水下场景中显著提升了鲁棒性和零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: AquaStereo 通过数据模拟与自蒸馏联合解决了水下立体匹配的数据稀缺与特征退化问题，为水下环境中的立体视觉提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Learning-based stereo matching models struggle in underwater environments due to scarce in-domain data and the difficulty of extracting discriminative correspondences from degraded imagery. In this work, we present $\textbf{AquaStereo}$, a perception-enhanced framework with a data simulation pipeline and a self-distillation strategy that jointly address data scarcity and feature degradation in underwater stereo matching. First, a depth-conditioned diffusion pipeline renders underwater stereo pairs while preserving binocular geometry, with a lightweight left-right consistency module ensuring geometric alignment. Training on this synthetic corpus effectively narrows the terrestrial-underwater gap and improves zero-shot robustness. Second, a frozen binocular teacher trained on clean terrestrial pairs guides a student exposed to rendered underwater pairs with perturbations. A stage-weighted sequence loss is performed to align the student's disparities with the teacher's geometry, while a clean-branch supervision with shared pseudo targets prevents scale drift. To further enhance feature stability under turbidity and low texture, we introduce learnable perception frames, a perception-enhanced feature formulation that constructs robust matching descriptors by fusing temporal cues from two auxiliary views encoded by a video backbone with semantic features extracted by a strong image encoder. Extensive experiments demonstrate that $\textbf{AquaStereo}$ substantially improves robustness and zero-shot generalization in challenging underwater scenarios. The code is available at https://github.com/qz-wei/AquaStereo.

</details>


### [210] [Road-Aware Anomaly Segmentation with Query-Guided Polygons and CLIP in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04304)
*Zhiran Yan,Gordon Elger*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级、后处理式的道路感知异常分割框架，基于掩码Transformer分割网络，利用查询级掩码置信度与多边形道路先验检测异常区域，并引入CLIP零样本语义过滤模块抑制误检。无需重训练、无需OOD数据或辅助监督，在多个基准数据集上表现优异，尤其Fishyscapes LostAndFound上AP最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
