We study this problem in the game of Rocket League, where players compete and cooperate under fast, tightly coupled dynamics. Trained on 10,000 hours of gameplay collected with publicly available bots, our 5-billion-parameter latent diffusion model generates four-player matches in real time, producing 20 frames per second on a single Nvidia B200 GPU. Although trained only on short clips, its rollouts stay stable far beyond the training horizon: distributional quality holds steady out to five minutes, the longest horizon we measure, and in practice we observe rollouts continuing for hours with no sign of collapse. We systematically investigate the central design choices: the video codec, the generative objective, and the multiplayer conditioning scheme. In addition, we characterize how behavior changes with model and data scale, including the capabilities that emerge and the failure modes that persist. We further develop targeted evaluations that probe the model's physical understanding rather than visual appearance alone. To support continued research on multiplayer world models, we release our dataset, our full training and inference codebase, and a live demo.

</details>


### [317] [Geometric Reciprocity: Unlocking Self-Supervision for Stereoscopic Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05354)
*Jingyi Lu,Kai Han*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自监督的单目转立体框架，通过几何互易定理（GRT）实现了无需立体数据或合成数据的训练，在测试时可直接从单目图像解析出遮挡掩码，显著优于现有训练无关和有监督方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于深度图像渲染（DIBR）的立体转换方法中，立体修复是瓶颈；监督方法依赖稀缺的立体对或合成数据，存在领域差距，亟需一种无需标注数据的高质量自监督方案。

Method: 提出几何互易定理（GRT）：在最近邻DIBR框架下，合成目标视图时的遮挡掩码等价于从目标视图反向映射回源视图时丢失像素的掩码，从而可直接从单目图像解析测试时的遮挡掩码，实现训练-测试一致性，支持从无限单目视频进行自监督学习。

Result: 在多个基准上显著优于训练无关方法（如传统修复算法）和有监督的SOTA方法，展示了自监督框架的优越性能。

Conclusion: 本文首次实现了基于自监督学习的单目转立体转换，通过GRT解决了训练数据依赖问题，为立体内容生成提供了一条无需标注数据的高质量新路径。

Abstract: Monocular-to-stereo conversion synthesizes stereoscopic content from 2D videos for immersive 3D experiences. In modern Depth-Image-Based Rendering (DIBR) approaches, stereo inpainting of disocclusions is the critical bottleneck. Training-based methods achieve superior quality but rely on scarce stereo pairs or synthetic data with domain gaps. We address this through the first self-supervised framework learning from monocular videos via cycle consistency. Our key contribution is the Geometric Reciprocity Theorem (GRT): under the nearest-neighbor DIBR formulation, the disocclusion mask when synthesizing a target view equals the mask of pixels lost when warping back from target to source, enabling analytical computation of test-time disocclusion masks directly from monocular images. This yields train-test consistency for the stated warping formulation, supporting self-supervised learning from unlimited monocular videos and substantial improvements over training-free and supervised state-of-the-art methods. Project page: https://visual-ai.github.io/grt/

</details>


### [318] [ReCal3R: Reliability-Calibrated Learning Rates for Streaming 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05356)
*Xinze Li,Yiyuan Wang,Pengxu Chen,Wentao Fan,Weifeng Su,Weisi Lin,Wentao Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReCal3R，一种面向流式3D重建的可靠性校准学习率方法，通过估计状态token可靠性来动态调整学习率，抑制不可靠token的激进更新，在长序列上显著提升位姿、深度和重建质量，ATE降低3.7倍，且不增加运行时和内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流式3D重建中，重复更新会逐渐破坏紧凑的循环场景状态，导致可靠的历史信息被噪声或模糊观测覆盖。现有方法缺乏对状态token可靠性的感知，统一使用候选学习率进行更新，无法区分可靠与不可靠的token。

Method: 提出基于可靠性校准的学习率方法：从维护的场景状态中估计每个状态token的可靠性，结合token对齐、状态重建残差和近期更新压力计算候选学习率，通过可靠性对候选学习率进行校准，在保守基础率与候选率之间插值，实现token维度的自适应学习率。

Result: 将ReCal3R作为无训练校准规则应用于CUT3R，在长序列上ATE降低3.7倍，位姿、深度和重建质量指标均有显著提升，运行时和内存开销与基线相当。

Conclusion: ReCal3R通过轻量级、无训练的可靠性校准机制，有效缓解了循环状态在长序列更新中的退化问题，为流式3D重建提供了一种高效且实用的改进方案。

Abstract: Streaming 3D reconstruction relies on a compact recurrent scene state to process long image streams in linear time and bounded memory. However, repeated updates can gradually corrupt this state, causing reliable historical information to be overwritten by noisy or ambiguous observations. We introduce ReCal3R, a reliability-calibrated learning rate method for recurrent 3D reconstruction. Instead of directly applying a candidate learning rate, our method estimates state token reliability from the maintained scene state and uses it to calibrate a candidate learning rate derived from token alignment, state reconstruction residual, and recent update pressure. The resulting token-wise learning rate interpolates between a conservative base rate and the candidate rate, suppressing aggressive updates on unreliable tokens while preserving adaptation to informative frames. Applied to CUT3R as a training-free calibration rule, ReCal3R reaches strong performance on long sequences in pose, depth, and reconstruction quality, including a 3.7$\times$ reduction in ATE, with comparable runtime and memory. Code is available at: https://github.com/Powertony102/ReCal3R.

</details>


### [319] [PixWorld: Unifying 3D Scene Generation and Reconstruction in Pixel Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05373)
*Sensen Gao,Zhaoqing Wang,Qihang Cao,Dongdong Yu,Changhu Wang,Jia-Wang Bian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PixWorld，一种在像素空间中对3D重建和生成进行统一建模的方法，通过在渲染图像上直接进行扩散监督，并引入几何感知损失来对齐3D结构，消除了潜在空间方法的信息损失和对预训练VAE/RAE的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D重建和生成任务分别采用不同的范式（重建用像素回归，生成用潜在扩散），虽有统一尝试但存在潜在特征信息损失、依赖预训练VAE/RAE、扩散目标定义在潜在特征而非3D表示上等问题。

Method: 提出统一像素空间扩散范式PixWorld：直接在渲染图像上监督扩散过程，消除潜在编码的信息损失；引入几何感知损失，利用预训练3D基础模型的几何感知特征空间对齐渲染视图与真实视图，提供3D结构监督。

Result: PixWorld在生成任务上持续优于先前的潜在空间方法，在重建任务上达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: 统一的像素空间方法优于潜在空间统一方法，能在单一模型中同时解决3D重建和生成问题，且无需依赖预训练VAE/RAE。

Abstract: 3D reconstruction and generation are commonly tackled by separate paradigms: pixel-based regression for reconstruction, and latent diffusion for generation. Recent works attempt to unify them in latent space, but with notable drawbacks: the diffusion objective is defined on latent features rather than the underlying 3D representation, and both branches suffer from information loss introduced by latent encoding, while requiring a pretrained Variational Autoencoder (VAE) or Representation Autoencoder (RAE). In this paper, we reformulate these two tasks under a unified pixel-space diffusion paradigm and introduce PixWorld, a single model that jointly addresses 3D reconstruction and generation. By supervising diffusion directly on rendered images, PixWorld removes the above limitations and aligns optimization with 3D scene fidelity. Beyond photometric and perceptual supervision that operates at the 2D image level and lacks 3D geometric awareness, we further introduce a geometry perception loss that aligns rendered views with their ground truth in the geometry-aware feature space of a pretrained 3D foundation model, providing 3D structural supervision. PixWorld consistently outperforms prior latent-space generation methods and matches state-of-the-art reconstruction methods, demonstrating the superiority of a unified pixel-space approach.

</details>


### [320] [MV-Forcing: Long Multi-View Video Generation via 4D-Grounded Spatio-Temporal Self-Forcing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05376)
*Gal Fiebelman,Hadar Averbuch-Elor,Sagie Benaim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MV-Forcing 是一个通过4D几何桥接在单扩散模型中组合时序与视角自回归的框架，能生成长序列、多视角一致的动态场景视频，并通过分布匹配蒸馏实现少步推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散模型要么支持长时序单视角生成（时序自回归），要么支持短多视角合成（双向注意力），但无法同时实现长序列多视角一致的动态场景视频生成。

Method: (1) 引入4D几何桥接：用自回归3D重建模型从已完成源视图重建3D结构，渲染下一目标视角的几何先验，供扩散模型精化为高质量视频；(2) 联合去噪机制：训练时将两个视角槽位从噪声初始化，实现时序无界生成；(3) 分布匹配蒸馏结合时空自强迫，弥合时序和视角自回归的推理-训练暴露偏差。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的实验表明，MV-Forcing 能以任意长度和视角数量生成几何一致的多视角动态场景视频，且仅需单步学生模型推理。

Conclusion: 通过4D几何桥接和蒸馏策略，首次在单扩散模型中统一了长时序与多视角的联合生成任务，兼顾了几何一致性与高效推理。

Abstract: Recent advances in video diffusion models have enabled either long single-view generation through temporal autoregression, or short multi-view synthesis through bidirectional attention. However, generating long, multi-view consistent videos of dynamic scenes remains unsolved. In this work, we present MV-Forcing, a framework that composes temporal and view-wise autoregression within a single diffusion model by introducing a 4D geometric bridge between sequentially generated views. Our key insight is that an autoregressive 3D reconstruction model naturally interfaces between autoregressively generated views. Given a completed source view, we reconstruct its 3D structure and render a geometric prior of the next target viewpoint, which the diffusion model refines into a high-quality video. To extend generation beyond the teacher's fixed temporal window, we introduce a joint denoising regime where both view slots are initialized from noise during training, enabling temporally unbounded generation. We distill the model via Distribution Matching Distillation with Spatio-Temporal Self-Forcing, closing the train-inference exposure bias gap for both temporal and view-sequential autoregression. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and real-world data demonstrate that MV-Forcing produces geometrically consistent multi-view videos of dynamic scenes at arbitrary lengths and viewpoint counts using a single few-step student model.

</details>


### [321] [Search Beyond What Can Be Taught: Evolving the Knowledge Boundary in Agentic Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05382)
*Haozhe Wang,Weijia Feng,Jinpeng Yu,Che Liu,Ping Nie,Fangzhen Lin,Jiaming Liu,Ruihua Huang,Jimmy Lin,Wenhu Chen,Cong Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SearchGen-20K基准和SearchGen-Corpus-1M检索语料库，揭示前沿视觉生成模型在长尾世界知识需求上存在40分的大幅性能下降（满分100仅得21-28分），并发现简单检索增强会引入噪声，因此提出一种"先教后搜"的协同训练框架来动态发现生成器的知识边界，实现递归自我改进的视觉生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉生成模型在训练集固定的情况下无法处理开放世界中无限长尾的用户需求（新角色、新实体、截止日期后事件等），而现有基准无法暴露这一问题。作者希望通过构建大规模长尾基准并探索检索增强方案，解决生成器"自信捏造未知内容"的结构性缺陷。

Method: 1) 构建SearchGen-20K基准（20,839条prompt，覆盖12类失败模式、22个领域）及SearchGen-Corpus-1M多模态检索语料库；2) 评估前沿开源生成器发现性能严重下降；3) 提出"teach-then-search"协同训练框架：先让模型学习自身知识边界，再针对性检索外部知识，实现单调改进和递归自我提升。

Result: 前沿开源生成器在SearchGen-Bench上仅得21-28分（满分100），相比现有基准出现约40分的性能崩塌。简单检索增强效果不佳，而提出的协同训练框架即使最简版本也能实现单调性能提升。

Conclusion: 视觉生成领域存在结构化世界知识瓶颈，简单检索增强不可行。通过可发现的生成器特定知识边界和协同训练框架，可以实现面向世界知识需求的工具增强视觉生成，为递归自我改进奠定基础。

Abstract: Visual generators excel at rendering, but they confidently fabricate what they do not know. User requests are unbounded, evolving, and deeply long-tailed: new characters, trending entities, post-cutoff events, and more. This world-knowledge bottleneck is structural: generators are trained on fixed corpora, but the visual world is open-ended. We construct SearchGen-20K and SearchGen-Bench, with 20,839 prompts spanning twelve failure categories and twenty-two domains, paired with a pre-executed multimodal SearchGen-Corpus-1M to support offline, reproducible research. On SearchGen-Bench, frontier open generators score only 21 to 28 out of 100, a 40-point collapse invisible to existing benchmarks. The natural remedy is to employ search tools, enabling agentic visual generation. However, we find that naive search fails: it retrieves indiscriminately, injecting noise into prompts the generator already handles. We trace the root cause to a generator-specific, evolving knowledge boundary: the divide between what a generator can internalize through training and what must remain in external context. Although this boundary is hard to specify in advance, we show that it is discoverable through a teach-then-search co-training framework. Even a minimal version of this co-training recipe produces monotonic improvement, laying the foundation for recursive self-improvement in visual generation that can meet world-knowledge-grounded requests. We release the full dataset, co-training corpus, and search corpus as a replayable harness for tool-augmented, world-knowledge-grounded visual generation.

</details>


### [322] [InFlux++: Real and Synthetic Data for Estimating Dynamic Camera Intrinsics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05389)
*Erich Liang,Caleb Kha-Uong,Chinmaya Saran,Sreemanti Dey,David W. Liu,Junhan Ouyang,Benjamin Zhou,Jia Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出InFlux++，包含大规模合成数据集InFlux++ Synth（441K+标注帧，1841个高分辨率视频，含精确内参、位姿、深度、法线）和真实世界基准InFlux++ Real（514K+帧，334个视频），用于动态相机内参估计。实验表明在合成数据上微调可提升焦距估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D算法通常假设视频中相机内参固定，但真实视频中内参常动态变化。已有的InFlux基准虽开创了动态内参研究，但存在训练数据稀缺、内参多样性不足以及基准场景和相机运动多样性有限的问题，导致现有方法精度不高。

Method: 构建两个组件：(1) InFlux++ Synth：大规模程序化生成的合成视频数据集，含441K+标注帧、1841个高分辨率视频，提供每帧精确内参真值，子集还提供位姿、深度和法线；视频包含变焦、对焦变化、动态物体以及镜头畸变、散焦模糊等真实感渲染效果。(2) InFlux++ Real：大规模真实世界基准，扩展InFlux至514K+帧、334个高分辨率视频，覆盖更广的场景和相机运动范围。

Result: 在InFlux++ Synth上微调现有内参预测方法，在InFlux++ Real和InFlux两个基准上均一致提升了焦距估计的准确性，表明合成监督对RGB内参预测具有潜力。

Conclusion: InFlux++有效解决了动态相机内参估计中训练数据稀缺和评估基准多样性不足的问题，合成数据监督是一种有前景的方向。

Abstract: Camera intrinsics are vital for recovering 3D structure from 2D video. However, most 3D algorithms assume fixed intrinsics throughout a video, an assumption that often fails for real-world in-the-wild videos. Consequently, estimating per-frame intrinsics from RGB images is critical for making 3D methods robust to videos with dynamic intrinsics. InFlux previously advanced this research direction by establishing the first real-world benchmark with per-frame ground truth intrinsics for dynamic intrinsics videos. Nevertheless, existing methods remain inaccurate due to two obstacles: (i) training data is scarce and lacks intrinsics diversity; and (ii) benchmarks, including InFlux, have limited scene and camera motion diversity, making it difficult to properly evaluate methods. To address both gaps, we present InFlux++, consisting of two components. InFlux++ Synth is a large-scale procedurally generated synthetic video dataset with 441K+ annotated frames from 1841 high-resolution videos, providing accurate per-frame ground truth intrinsics for training dynamic intrinsics prediction models; a subset also includes per-frame pose, depth, and normals. The videos feature rich intrinsics diversity through changes in camera zoom and focus, as well as dynamic objects and realistic rendering effects such as lens distortion and defocus blur. InFlux++ Real is a large-scale real-world benchmark that extends InFlux with 514K+ newly captured frames across 334 high-resolution videos, spanning a wider range of scenes and camera motions. Finetuning existing intrinsics prediction methods on InFlux++ Synth consistently improves focal length estimation across both InFlux++ Real and InFlux, suggesting that synthetic supervision is promising for RGB-based intrinsics prediction. For the dataset, benchmark, code, videos, submission instructions, and live leaderboard, please visit https://influx.cs.princeton.edu/ .

</details>


### [323] [SynCity 3000: Bootstrapping Scene-Scale 3D Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05392)
*Paul Engstler,Iro Laina,Christian Rupprecht,Andrea Vedaldi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SynCity 3000是一个通过将图像到3D生成器改造为卷积算子、并利用合成数据引擎微调，从而从单个提示生成全局一致且布局可控的大规模3D场景的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像到3D生成器能生成复杂3D资产，但无法扩展到整个场景级别；同时缺乏用于训练的3D场景数据。

Method: 1) 提出合成数据引擎生成场景级数据以解决数据稀缺问题；2) 将图像到3D生成器微调并适配为卷积算子；3) 对用户提示生成的等轴测图像应用该卷积生成器，输出任意规模和复杂度的3D场景。

Result: 在多种提示和布局下，SynCity 3000能够生成大规模、连贯且细节丰富的3D场景，优于先前的3D场景生成方法。

Conclusion: SynCity 3000通过卷积化生成器和合成数据引擎，有效解决了3D场景生成中的全局一致性和细粒度布局控制问题。

Abstract: We present SynCity 3000, a framework for generating 3D scenes that are globally coherent while enabling fine-grained layout control. Building on the ability of current image-to-3D generators to produce complex 3D assets from a single image, we extend this capability to the scale of entire scenes by adapting the generator to be applicable as a convolutional operator. We achieve this by fine-tuning the model on scene-like data generated by a new synthetic data engine, which we propose to address the scarcity of 3D scene data for training. The convolutional generator is then applied to a dimetric image of the entire scene, generated from the user prompt, resulting in 3D scenes of arbitrary size and complexity. Across diverse prompts and layouts, SynCity 3000 produces large, coherent, and detailed scenes, addressing the shortcomings of prior approaches to 3D scene generation.

</details>


### [324] [From Fixed to Free Cameras: Calibration-Free View-Robust Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05396)
*Wenhao Li,Xueying Jiang,Quanhao Qian,Deli Zhao,Shijian Lu,Gongjie Zhang,Ran Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CamVLA，一种无需相机标定、深度信息或额外视角的视角鲁棒视觉-语言-动作策略。它通过预测相机坐标系下的末端执行器动作和手眼矩阵，将操控控制与相机几何解耦，从而在训练后适应任意相机视角变化，在仿真和真实机器人数据上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视角鲁棒的VLA策略在相机外参显式提供时才能容忍视角变化，这在实际部署中脆弱且难以使用。作者认为策略不应被告知相机位置，而应自主推断。

Method: 提出CamVLA，预测两个解耦输出：(i)相机坐标系下的末端执行器动作，(ii)相机到机器人基座的6-DoF手眼矩阵。通过确定性几何变换将两者组合为基座坐标系动作，实现姿态无关的动作生成与视角相关的几何基座解耦。仅需单目RGB图像和任务指令。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人数据上，CamVLA在不同未见过的视角下均一致提升了任务成功率。

Conclusion: CamVLA实现了免标定、免深度、单视图的视角鲁棒VLA策略，有效解决了实际部署中相机视角变化问题。

Abstract: Real-world robot deployment rarely maintains the training-stage camera setup, where cameras often experience repositioning or remounting depending on actual scenarios. Existing view-robust Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies tolerate such camera variations only when the camera extrinsics are explicitly provided, making them fragile and hard to use especially when view robustness is critical. We argue that the policy should not be told where the camera is, but rather figure it out by itself. To this end, we introduce Camera-Centric VLA (CamVLA), a new VLA model that decouples manipulation controls from camera geometry by predicting (i) a camera-centric end-effector action expressed in the local camera frame, and (ii) a 6-DoF hand-eye matrix relating cameras to the robot base. A deterministic geometric transformation composes the two predictions into a robot base-frame action. This disentangles how I should move in pose-independent camera-centric action generation from where I am looking from in camera-perspective geometric grounding. The resulting policy is calibration-free, depth-free, and single-view, requiring only a single monocular RGB image as the visual observation and task instruction at deployment. Evaluations in both simulation and real-world robot data show that CamVLA consistently improves success rates across diverse unseen viewpoints. Project page: https://alibaba-damo-academy.github.io/CamVLA/.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [325] [Improving LLMs via Validator-to-Generator Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02668)
*Juan Diego Rodriguez,Jocelyn Zhang,Katrin Erk,Greg Durrett*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出频率校正生成器-验证器一致性（FCPA）方法，通过修正词频偏差来训练大语言模型，显著提升生成器与验证器之间的一致性，在IFEval和HumanEval上Pearson相关最多提升27个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM存在不一致性问题，其中生成器-验证器（G-V）差距表现为模型生成的内容被自身验证为无效。简单的一致性度量因生成器常对有效字符串赋予低似然（因其先验概率低）而无法直接使用。

Method: 提出频率校正生成器-验证器一致性（FCPA）训练目标，基于理性代理回答多答案问题的自然模型，对生成器分数进行频率校正后与验证器保持一致。

Result: 在IFEval和HumanEval上Pearson相关最多提升27个百分点，在保留验证器质量的同时显著提升G-V一致性和生成器性能。

Conclusion: 频率校正是实现G-V一致性的关键，FCPA训练目标能有效缓解LLM的不一致性问题。

Abstract: Large language models are inconsistent: varying prompts or including unrelated information can lead to unexpected changes in model outputs. The generator-validator (G-V) gap is one manifestation of this phenomenon, where LLMs generate responses that they then deem as invalid if re-queried to validate them. In this work, we introduce a new formulation of G-V consistency that involves a principled correction for utterance frequency. Specifically, generators often assign low likelihood to valid strings simply because those strings are a priori unlikely, which makes naive notions of G-V consistency unworkable. We show that under a natural model of rational agents answering questions with multiple answers, consistency of the validator with a frequency-corrected generator score emerges naturally. Our method, \emph{\FCPAname} (\FCPA), is a training objective implementing frequency-corrected G-V consistency for real-world LLMs. Our experimental results show that training with \FCPA{} substantially improves both G-V consistency and generator performance over prior methods, with gains of up to $+27$pp in Pearson correlation on IFEval and HumanEval, while preserving validator quality across all evaluated tasks.

</details>


### [326] [Echoes of Unrest: A Multimodal NLP Framework for Early Warning of Fake News and Violence-Driven Mob Activity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02734)
*Md. Maruf Bangabashi,Tahmid Hasan,Golam Mahmud,Md. Mostafijur Rahman,Md. Toufiqur Rahman,Jahanur Biswas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个多语言、多模态的NLP框架，融合XLM-RoBERTa、CLIP和多头注意力机制，在138,256条孟加拉语和英语样本上实现98%的测试准确率，用于早期检测 misinformation 和暴力倾向动态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体中虚假信息、操纵内容和煽动性叙事的快速传播已导致社会动荡、政治不稳定和群体暴力事件，尤其在 South Asia 地区。事实核查速度远不及虚假信息扩散速度，亟需自动化的早期检测框架。

Method: 构建包含138,256条孟加拉语和英语样本的融合数据集；框架集成XLM-RoBERTa进行多语言文本表示、CLIP进行视觉嵌入、多头注意力机制进行多模态融合，并加入讽刺检测和地理空间元数据等辅助特征。

Result: 在30%分层测试子集上达到98%的测试准确率，精确率和召回率均表现优异。

Conclusion: 多模态方法在早期 misinformation 检测中效果显著，地理空间信号对预测真实世界的事态升级具有额外价值。

Abstract: Rapid growth in social media has transformed global communication by enabling fast information exchange, but it has also accelerated the spread of misinformation. Fake news, manipulated content, and provocative narratives are increasingly linked to social unrest, political instability, and mob violence. Incidents in South Asia and elsewhere demonstrate how false information disseminated via platforms such as Facebook and WhatsApp can trigger real-world harm, often spreading faster than fact-checking efforts can respond. To address this challenge, this chapter presents a multilingual, multimodal Natural Language Processing (NLP) framework for early detection of misinformation and violence-prone dynamics. A fused dataset of 138,256 Bangla and English samples was created by combining multiple benchmark datasets. The framework integrates XLM-RoBERTa for multilingual text representation, CLIP for visual embedding, and a multi-head attention mechanism for multimodal fusion, enhanced with auxiliary features such as sarcasm and geospatial metadata. Experiments on a stratified 30% subset achieved 98% test accuracy with strong precision and recall. The outcomes show the efficacy of multimodal approaches in early misinformation detection and highlight the added value of geospatial signals for anticipating real-world escalation.

</details>


### [327] [Reinforcement Learning for Data-Efficient Code-Switched ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02757)
*Ziwei Ye,Peter Vickers*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR）的编码转换语音识别方法，使用群体相对策略优化（GRPO），结合错误率奖励和脚本保真度奖励，在仅用10%数据的情况下匹配全数据集LoRA监督微调性能，并在10种语言对和零样本迁移上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音频语言模型在编码转换语音识别中解码未针对语码转换优化，常在语言边界处失效，需要一种数据高效的适配方法。

Method: 提出RLVR（强化学习+可验证奖励）方案，采用GRPO算法，结合两类奖励函数：错误率奖励（消除翻译错误）和脚本保真度奖励（惩罚错误书写系统）；并使用两遍草稿-精炼（draft-and-refinement）流程。仅用TTS合成的编码转换语音训练。

Result: 在Qwen2-Audio上评估10种语言对，仅用10%数据的RLVR训练即可匹配全数据集LoRA监督微调性能；在类型距离远的语言对上增益最大；错误率奖励消除翻译错误，脚本保真度奖励减少脚本污染；零样本迁移到真实人类录音语料库。

Conclusion: RLVR是一种数据高效的编码转换ASR适配方法，通过分离优化目标（错误率和脚本保真度）实现更好的解码质量，且具有良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Audio-language models can be prompted for code-switched speech, but their decoding is not optimized for code-switching and often fails at language boundaries. We propose a practical reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards recipe for data-efficient adaptation of audio-language models to code-switched ASR using group relative policy optimization, combining an error rate reward with a script fidelity reward that penalizes wrong writing systems and a two-pass draft-and-refinement procedure. Using Qwen2-Audio as a reproducible testbed across 10 language pairs, training on only TTS code-switched speech, we show that RLVR with 10% of the data matches LoRA supervised fine-tuning trained on the full dataset, with the largest gains on typologically distant pairs. The error rate reward eliminates translation errors while the script fidelity reward separately reduces script contamination without degradation. These gains transfer zero-shot to a human-recorded code-switching corpus.

</details>


### [328] [LuxSQA: Ask Me in Luxembourgish with TTS-Augmented Spoken Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02763)
*Nina Hosseini-Kivanani,Marco Matassoni,Alessio Brutti*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究利用TTS生成合成语音数据，在没有大规模人工标注语料的情况下，构建卢森堡语口语问答系统。通过多源TTS合成约23万条语音问题，结合冻结的Whisper编码器和多语言LLM后端，在真实说话人条件下取得了最佳性能，并发现TTS语音质量与下游QA性能并非单调正相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有口语问答研究集中在高资源语言和精心录制的语音上，低资源语言缺乏大规模人工标注的QA语音语料库，限制了语音-LLM方法的应用。本文旨在探索是否可以利用TTS生成任务特定的训练数据，在不依赖大规模人工录制的情况下解决低资源语言SQA问题。

Method: 1) 从现有文本QA资源出发，将问题翻译成卢森堡语；2) 使用多种TTS系统（MMS-TTS、Qwen3-TTS、OmniVoice变体）合成语音问题，构建单源（~48k）和多源混合（~230k）数据集；3) 训练参数高效的SLAM风格架构，连接冻结的Whisper编码器和冻结的多语言LLM后端，中间通过可学习的投影器和LoRA适配器进行桥接。

Result: 在LLAMA-LB-Test数据集上，两种真实卢森堡语说话人条件下的实验表明，多源和基于语音设计的合成训练配置取得了最强的SQA性能。此外，无参考TTS质量评分与下游QA性能之间并非单调正相关关系。

Conclusion: TTS生成的合成语音可以作为低资源语言SQA的有效训练数据，多源混合策略优势明显；合成语音作为任务特定训练数据的评估方式应区别于传统的自然度评价，不能仅依赖TTS质量分数来预测下游任务表现。

Abstract: Spoken Question Answering (SQA) remains largely focused on high-resource languages and carefully recorded speech, limiting the reach of speech-LLM methods in low-resource settings. This paper investigates whether text-to-speech (TTS) can provide task-specific training data for Luxembourgish SQA without requiring a large human-recorded QA corpus. Starting from existing text-based QA resources, we translate questions into Luxembourgish, synthesize spoken questions with multiple TTS systems, and pair them with textual answers. We train a parameter-efficient SLAM-style architecture that connects a frozen Whisper encoder to frozen multilingual LLM backends through a learned projector and LoRA adapters. We compare MMS-TTS, Qwen3-TTS, and OmniVoice variants, including single-source corpora of about 48k questions and a 4TTS multi-source mix of approximately 230k questions. Evaluation on LLAMA-LB-Test with two real Luxembourgish speaker conditions shows that multi-source and voice-design-based synthetic training configurations yield the strongest SQA performance. The results also show that no-reference TTS quality scores do not monotonically predict downstream QA performance, indicating that synthetic speech must be evaluated as task-specific training data rather than only as natural-sounding audio.

</details>


### [329] [Gemma 4 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02770)
*Gemma Team,Sherif El Abd,Vaibhav Aggarwal,Robin Algayres,Alek Andreev,Olivier Bachem,Ian Ballantyne,Cormac Brick,Victor Cărbune,Michelle Casbon,Mayank Chaturvedi,Victor Cotruta,Alice Coucke,Phil Culliton,Robert Dadashi,Lucas Dixon,Mohamed Elhawaty,Utku Evci,Clément Farabet,Johan Ferret,Filippo Galgani,Sertan Girgin,Jean-Bastien Grill,Maarten Grootendorst,Jiaxian Guo,Cassidy Hardin,Yanzhang He,Steven M. Hernandez,Omri Homburger,Léonard Hussenot,Juyeong Ji,Armand Joulin,Aishwarya Kamath,Parnian Kassraie,Olivier Lacombe,Preethi Lahoti,Gaël Liu,Gus Martins,Luciano Martins,Tatiana Matejovicova,Ramona Merhej,Nikola Momchev,Sneha Mondal,Ryan Mullins,Sindhu Raghuram Panyam,Shreya Pathak,Sarah Perrin,André Susano Pinto,Etienne Pot,Angéline Pouget,Alexandre Ramé,Sabela Ramos,Douglas Reid,David Rim,Morgane Rivière,Karsten Roth,Louis Rouillard,Omar Sanseviero,Pier Giuseppe Sessa,Shane Settle,Danila Sinopalnikov,Sara Smoot,Piotr Stanczyk,Andreas Steiner,Lawrence Stewart,Ilya Tolstikhin,Michael Tschannen,Anton Tsitsulin,Nino Vieillard,Renjie Wu,Pingmei Xu,Haichuan Yang,Edouard Yvinec,Li Zhang,Joe Zou,Nicolas Aagnes,Abdelrahman Abdelhamed,Shivani Agrawal,Shubham Agrawal,Ibrahim Alabdulmohsin,Jean Baptiste Alayrac,Uri Alon,Chandramouli Amarnath,Ankesh Anand,Chrysovalantis Anastasiou,Setareh Ariafar,François-Xavier Aubet,Kyriakos Axiotis,Federico Barbero,Joelle Barral,Alexei Bendebury,Urs Bergmann,Stanley Bileschi,Kat Black,Mathieu Blondel,Sebastian Borgeaud,Arthur Bražinskas,Ryan Burnell,Robert Busa-Fekete,Mu Cai,Glenn Cameron,Charlotte Caucheteux,Garima Chadha,Jetha Chan,Aditya Chawla,Blake Jianhang Chen,Jesse Chen,Lin Chen,Xu Chen,Derek Cheng,Tzu-hsiang Chien,Nikolai Chinaev,Yi Chou,Zhaohui Chu,Benjamin Coleman,Pooja Consul,Sam Conway-Rahman,Scott Crowell,Dylan Cutler,Vivek Dani,Samira Daruki,Anil Das,Daniel Deutsch,Nishanth Dikkala,Li Ding,Qiuhan Ding,Shenil Dodhia,Konstantin Donhauser,Tulsee Doshi,Anca Dragan,Alex Druinsky,Sahil Dua,Zoltan Egyed,Danielle Eisenbud,Daniel Eppens,Cindy Fan,Bahare Fatemi,Yassir Fathullah,Vlad Feinberg,Milen Ferev,Takumi Fujimoto,Isaac Galatzer-Levy,João Gante,Simon Geisler,Soham Ghosal,Antonious M. Girgis,Alec Go,Alhaad Gokhale,Alex Grills,Yiming Gu,Pramod Gupta,Guru Guruganesh,Raia Hadsell,Hamza Harkous,Jitendra Harlalka,Demis Hassabis,Anja Hauth,Joe Heyward,Arian Hosseini,Chih-Yang Hsia,I-Hung Hsu,Xiaopeng Huang,Yangsibo Huang,Kevin Hui,Adrian Hutter,Te I,Fotis Iliopoulos,Advait Jain,Ganesh Jawahar,Ziwei Ji,Qilin Jin,Melvin Johnson,Kandarp Joshi,Arun Kandoor,Wang-Cheng Kang,Koray Kavukcuoglu,Mehran Kazemi,Kathleen Kenealy,Amr Khalifa,Phoebe Kirk,Suraj Kothawade,Vitaly Kovalev,Neel Kovelamudi,Adam Kraft,Ravin Kumar,Harish Kuppam,Justin Lannin,Chen-Yu Lee,Seungji Lee,Dmitry Lepikhin,Dongdong Li,Qiujia Li,Valentin Liévin,Ethan Lin,Ziqian Lin,Casper Liu,Tianlin Liu,Tianqi Liu,Xin Liu,Mayank Lunayach,Min Ma,Gagan Madan,Andrii Maksai,Eric Malmi,Michal Matuszak,Daniel McDuff,Gaurav Menghani,Daniil Mirylenka,Karolis Misiunas,Vedant Misra,Andreea Mitran,Kareem Mohamed,Maksim Mukha,Eric Noland,James O'Donnell,Kate Olszewska,Bernett Orlando,Wanqiong Pan,Rina Panigrahy,Unnati Parekh,Chunjong Park,Eric Paskie,Liqian Peng,Bryce Petrini,Slav Petrov,Jonas Pfeiffer,Bilal Piot,Martyna Plomecka,Siim Poder,Octavio Ponce,Arijit Pramanik,David Racz,Anish Rajan,Michelle Ramanovich,Anand Rao,Marvin Ritter,Vitor Rodrigues,Evan Rosen,Mikołaj Rybiński,Noveen Sachdeva,Michaël E. Sander,Rohit Sathyanarayana,Sagar Savla,Samuel Schmidgall,Tal Schuster,Benoit Seguin,Andrew Sellergren,Aliaksei Severyn,Izhak Shafran,Dhruv Shah,Yuan Shangguan,Ashish Shenoy,Pradeep Shenoy,Rakesh Shivanna,Pauline Sho,Lucas Spangher,Wojciech Stokowiec,Tim Strother,Yao Su,Yinghao Sun,Mukund Sundararajan,Andrea Tacchetti,Mor Hazan Taege,Pouya Tafti,Chetan Tekur,Rahul Thapa,Madeleine Traverse,Lenart Treven,Tao Tu,Chien Te Tung,Petar Veličković,Malini Pooni Venkat,Sagar Gubbi Venkatesh,Vidya Venkiteswaran,Francesco Visin,Alex Vitvitskyi,Kiran Vodrahalli,Weiyi Wang,Xin Wang,Tris Warkentin,Jan Wassenberg,John Wieting,Lechao Xiao,Hao Xu,Yuhui Xu,Fuzhao Xue,Arun Yadav,Jun Yan,Antoine Yang,Lin Yang,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Ziyu Ying,Jae Hyeon Yoo,Sajjad Zafar,Fred Zhang,Jiageng Zhang,Jianyi Zhang,Xiaofan Zhang,Chao Zhao,David Zhou,Chen Zou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Gemma 4 是新一代开源、原生多模态语言模型系列，涵盖密集和MoE架构（2.3B-31B参数），采用统一的无编码器架构、思维模式推理以及改进的视觉/音频编码器，在STEM、多模态和长上下文基准上取得显著性能提升，媲美更大的前沿开源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动计算效率和推理能力的发展，在开源模型中实现多模态、长上下文和推理能力的新突破。

Method: 提出了密集和MoE架构（2.3B-31B参数）；对全尺寸模型使用改进的视觉和音频编码器；为12B模型提出统一的无编码器架构，直接处理原始音频和图像块；集成思维模式以在响应前生成推理轨迹；在推理速度、内存和计算效率以及长上下文能力上进行了关键设计优化。

Result: 在STEM、多模态和长上下文基准上实现了性能飞跃，在人工评估任务中可与更大的前沿开源模型竞争。

Conclusion: Gemma 4 通过架构创新和效率优化，在开源多模态语言模型领域树立了新标杆。

Abstract: We introduce Gemma 4, a new generation of open-weight, natively multimodal language models in the Gemma model family. Designed to advance compute efficiency and reasoning, the Gemma 4 model suite features dense and Mixture-of-Experts architectures, ranging from 2.3B to 31B parameters. Alongside improved vision and audio encoders for all model sizes, we propose a unified, encoder-free architecture for our 12B model, which ingests raw audio and image patches. Furthermore, we integrate a thinking mode, enabling Gemma models to generate reasoning traces prior to responding. We improve inference speed, memory, and compute efficiency, as well as long-context abilities through critical design choices. Gemma 4 establishes a leap in performance across STEM, multimodal, and long-context benchmarks, and rivals larger, frontier open models in human-rated tasks.

</details>


### [330] [Seduced by the Narrative: Assessing Rule Adherence in Semi-Open Textual Sandboxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02802)
*Weiying Chen,Junlong Shen,Zhanyuan Guo,Xiaoou Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CoC-Seduce，一个基于TRPG机制的多智能体对抗基准，用于评估LLM作为裁判时对抗修辞注入攻击的鲁棒性。实验发现模型规模和显式推理机制均不能保证裁判鲁棒性，伪逻辑是最主要的攻击向量，跨文化设定暴露出系统性知识缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被部署为半开放文本游戏环境中的自主裁判时，用户意图可能与系统规则冲突，而模型天生倾向于帮助和顺从，容易受到修辞注入攻击（如伪逻辑推理和权威性胁迫）的绕过。

Method: 基于TRPG机制构建多智能体对抗基准CoC-Seduce，使用GPT-5.4、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Gemini 3.5 Flash三个前沿模型作为对抗生成器，在4个世界设定和16个技能类别下生成5,376个样本，并对20个目标裁判模型进行基准测试。

Result: 20个模型的评估显示，模型规模和显式推理机制均不能可靠地赋予裁判鲁棒性；伪逻辑是最主要的攻击向量；跨文化设定暴露了所有评估模型家族的系统性知识缺口。

Conclusion: 当前LLM作为裁判在对抗性修辞注入面前普遍脆弱，需要新的鲁棒性提升方法，尤其是在跨文化情境下。

Abstract: As LLMs are increasingly deployed as autonomous adjudicators in semi-open textual game environments, robust rule adherence becomes critical when user intent conflicts with system rules. However, these models are trained to be helpful and compliant, leaving them vulnerable to a class of attacks we term \textit{Rhetorical Injection}, where adversarial users exploit narrative framing techniques such as pseudo-logical reasoning and authoritative coercion to bypass adjudication logic. We present CoC-Seduce, a multi-agent adversarial benchmark built on Tabletop Role-Playing Game (TRPG) mechanics, an ideal instantiation of semi-open environments where rules are explicit for adjudication, yet interaction remains entirely in natural language. Three frontier models, i.e., GPT-5.4, Claude Sonnet 4.6, Gemini 3.5 Flash, serve as adversarial generators producing 5,376 samples across 4 world settings and 16 skill categories. We then benchmark 20 target adjudicators against this corpus. Evaluation across 20 models reveals that neither model scale nor explicit reasoning mechanisms reliably confer adjudication robustness, with \textsc{Pseudo-Logic} emerging as the dominant attack vector and cross-cultural settings exposing systematic knowledge gaps across all evaluated families. Project page: https://github.com/answerrtx/CoC-Seduce

</details>


### [331] [Jointly Improving Dialect Identification and ASR in Indian Languages using Multimodal Feature Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02862)
*Saurabh Kumar,Amartyaveer,Prasanta Kumar Ghosh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种多模态联合优化框架，通过瓶颈编码器提取方言特征、RoBERTa处理ASR嵌入、门控机制融合及注意力编码器精炼，在8种印度语言33种方言上实现DID准确率81.63%，CER 4.65%，WER 17.73%，有效提升了ASR与方言识别的联合性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法分别优化ASR或DID，导致性能权衡，而印度语言低资源且方言差异显著，亟需联合优化方案。

Method: 采用多模态框架：瓶颈编码器从Conformer语音表示中提取方言特征，RoBERTa编码器处理ASR生成的CTC嵌入，门控机制融合特征，注意力编码器精炼表示，最终将学习到的嵌入与Conformer输出拼接以增强ASR特征。

Result: 在8种印度语言33种方言上，DID准确率81.63%，平均CER 4.65%，平均WER 17.73%。

Conclusion: 该多模态联合框架有效解决了ASR与DID的性能权衡问题，在低资源印度语言方言场景下表现出色。

Abstract: Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) and Dialect Identification (DID) are crucial for Indian languages, many of which are low-resource and exhibit significant dialectal differences. Existing methods often optimize ASR or DID individually, resulting in performance trade-offs. In this work, we propose a multimodal framework that jointly improves ASR and DID. Our method employs a Bottleneck Encoder to extract dialectal features from Conformer-based speech representations and a RoBERTa encoder to process ASR-generated CTC embeddings. A gating mechanism merges these features, followed by an attention encoder to refine the representations. The learned embeddings are concatenated with Conformer outputs to enhance ASR features. Evaluated on eight Indian languages with thirty-three dialects, our method achieves an average DID accuracy of 81.63% and average CER and WER of 4.65% and 17.73%, respectively. These results highlight the effectiveness of our method for joint ASR-DID modeling.

</details>


### [332] [PraMem: Practice-derived Experiential Memory for Long-horizon Behavior Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02881)
*Zhuoqun Li,Boxi Cao,Jiawei Chen,Hanshu Zhou,Ruoxi Xu,Guiping Jiang,Ruotong Pan,Tingting Gao,Han Li,Xiangyu Wu,Hongyu Lin,Yaojie Lu,Xianpei Han,Le Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PraMem方法，通过将长历史序列从负担转变为有价值的资源，构建经验记忆以辅助LLM进行长程行为预测，实验证明该方法优于现有技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在处理长程行为预测时面临潜在行为模式归纳困难以及模型内在认知偏差的挑战，现有记忆管理方法采用上下文压缩范式试图减轻历史序列负担但未能解决核心问题。

Method: 提出PraMem，转变范式将长历史序列视为可开发资源而非负担，通过对长历史序列进行事先练习（beforehand practice）构建经验记忆，作为辅助输入用于准确的长程行为预测。

Result: 在多种不同任务上的广泛实验表明，PraMem优于先前方法，深入分析揭示了经验记忆的机制和演化过程。

Conclusion: 将历史序列从负担转化为资源并构建经验记忆是提升LLM长程行为预测能力的有效途径。

Abstract: Long-horizon behavior prediction aims to infer a user's next action based on a lengthy historical sequence, playing a crucial role in artificial intelligence field. The rise of large language models (LLMs) offers a promising direction for sequential behavior prediction, yet LLMs struggle with latent behavioral pattern induction and model-intrinsic cognitive biases when tackling long-horizon behavior prediction. Prior memory management methods follow a context-compression paradigm that attempts to address this task by alleviating the historical sequence burden, yet fail to resolve the core challenges. In this paper, we advocate a paradigm shift that reframes the lengthy historical sequence from a burden into a valuable resource to be exploited, and accordingly propose PraMem, which conducts beforehand practice over the lengthy historical sequence to build an experiential memory, thereby serving as the assisted input for accurate long-horizon behavior prediction. Extensive experiments across diverse tasks demonstrate that PraMem achieves superior performance than prior methods, and more in-depth analyses provide valuable insights into the mechanism and evolution of the experiential memory. Code: https://github.com/icip-cas/PraMem.

</details>


### [333] [Where do LLMs Fall Short in CBT-Guided Affective Reasoning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02885)
*Vaishnavi Sinha,Pooja Guttal,Pranay Deep Reddy Katike,Vishal Sinha,Gerald Ndawula,Lira Yoon,Andrea Kleinsmith,Manas Gaur*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLMs虽精通CBT理论知识（考试准确率达96%），但在实际对话中仍倾向于无差别的情感验证与反映。本文提出知识引导框架，将CBT对话视为受控情感推理，通过Beck认知概念化分解、SNOMED CT临床概念验证及多链思维策略选择来引导模型行为。引入协议杠杆力F作为行为级度量指标，发现单链思维提示几乎无法改变LLM行为，而多链思维虽有改进但效果仅约1.2-1.3%，模型仍偏向验证与反映策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs具备CBT理论知识但无法在实际对话中有效应用，存在理论与实践的显著脱节，需要可量化的框架来理解并测量这一差距。

Method: 提出知识引导框架：(1) Beck认知概念化分解用户叙事；(2) SNOMED CT临床概念通过自然语言推理验证；(3) 多链思维在验证与反映、苏格拉底式提问、替代视角三种策略间选择。引入协议杠杆力F作为行为级度量指标。

Result: 在三个开源LLM和14个RealCBT案例研究中，经人类专家评估、效价-唤醒轨迹和语言熵分析，单链思维提示无法改变LLM行为，多链思维可改善策略选择，但效果仅1.2-1.3%，所有模型仍偏向验证与反映。

Conclusion: CBT知识本身不足以确保有效应用，需要更强大的机制来改变LLM在情感对话中的默认行为模式，所提出的协议杠杆力F为情感计算社区提供了量化LLM不足的测量工具。

Abstract: Cognitive Behavioral Therapy (CBT) provides a structured framework for understanding a user's mental state by examining the interaction between cognitive and behavioral factors. However, out-of-the-box LLMs respond fluently and empathetically, yet collapse into validation & reflection, regardless of what the user actually needs. They know theoretical CBT (scoring up to 96% accuracy on licensing exam questions) but fail to apply it effectively. We explore this gap with a knowledge-guided framework that treats CBT dialogue as controlled affective reasoning: user narratives are decomposed into Beck's Cognitive Conceptualization structure, grounded in clinical SNOMED CT concepts validated via Natural Language Inference, and a Multiple Chain-of-Thought (MCoT) strategy selection between Validation & Reflection, Socratic Questioning, or Alternative Perspectives. To measure whether such guidance actually changes behavior, we introduce the Protocol Leverage Force (F), a behavior-level metric that captures how far an intervention shifts a model away from its default response. Across three open-weight LLMs and 14 RealCBT-derived case studies, evaluated with human experts, valence-arousal trajectories, and linguistic entrainment, F shows that simply introducing protocol definitions via single chain-of-thought prompting fails to change LLM behavior, while MCoT on these definitions guides strategy selection better. Still, the effect stays within 1% (approx. 1.2-1.3%), and all models remain biased toward Validation & Reflection. These results show CBT knowledge alone does not ensure effective application, giving the affective-computing community instrumentation to measure where LLMs fall short.

</details>


### [334] [Distill Where the Student Goes: Teacher-Regularized RL for English-Evidence Cross-Lingual RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02966)
*Haotian Zhou,Weiran Huang,Siqi Liu,Xiting Wang,Xin Zhang,Zhihao Wen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了TR-RAG，一种教师正则化强化学习框架，用于解决跨语言检索增强生成中的语言漂移和证据不可靠使用问题。通过奖励优化与on-policy蒸馏相结合，在多个基准上提升了语言一致性和证据正确性，并防止了奖励-only RL的语言崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨语言RAG中，即使基础模型强大，英文证据仍会导致语言漂移（输出英文或代码切换）以及生成非英文答案时证据使用不可靠。现有后训练方法面临两个挑战：前缀依赖导致固定轨迹监督存在前缀不匹配；序列级奖励（部分离散/基于评判器）带来噪声信用分配和高方差更新。

Method: 提出TR-RAG，包含：1) 紧凑学生模型在on-policy前缀上采样答案；2) 更强冻结教师仅在这些前缀上被查询，提供前缀级学生到教师的reverse-KL锚点；3) 针对英文证据多语言生成的奖励分解，包含语言一致性、字符3-gram召回率和LLM评判器证据正确性分数。

Result: 在BioASQ-ENKB5、Hotpot-ENKB5和MKQA三个基准以及两个骨干模型上，TR-RAG在语言一致性和证据正确性的复合指标上优于强基线。教师锚点作为安全网：在分布内语言上防止了高达约27个百分点的大幅语言一致性崩溃；在分布外语言上，当reward-only RL停滞时仍能提升证据接地性；紧凑学生在字符3-gram召回率上有时超越了其70B教师。

Conclusion: TR-RAG通过teacher-regularized RL有效解决了跨语言RAG中语言漂移和证据不可靠问题，教师锚点提供了关键的安全机制，防止强化学习导致的语言一致性崩溃，同时提升了证据接地的准确性。

Abstract: Cross-lingual retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is often deployed in an English-evidence regime, where users query in diverse languages but retrieved passages remain English. In this setting, generation can fail despite strong base models: English evidence induces language drift (English or code-switching outputs) and models use evidence unreliably when producing non-English answers. We attribute these failures to two post-training challenges: (i) errors are prefix-dependent, so fixed-trajectory supervision suffers from prefix mismatch; and (ii) sequence-level (partly discrete / judge-based) rewards yield noisy credit assignment and high-variance updates. We propose TR-RAG, a teacher-regularized RL recipe that couples reward optimization with on-policy distillation on student-visited prefixes. A compact student samples on-policy answers, while a stronger frozen teacher is queried only on those prefixes and provides a prefix-wise student-to-teacher reverse-KL anchor. We further introduce a reward decomposition for English-evidence multilingual generation, combining language consistency, character 3-gram recall, and an LLM-judge score for evidence-grounded correctness. Across three benchmarks -- BioASQ-ENKB5, Hotpot-ENKB5, and naturally multilingual MKQA -- and two backbones, TR-RAG improves the composite of language adherence and evidence-grounded correctness over strong baselines. Crucially, the teacher anchor acts as a safety net: on in-domain languages it prevents the large language-consistency collapses (up to ~27 percentage points) that reward-only RL can suffer by drifting below even the base model, while on distant out-of-distribution languages -- where reward-only RL stalls at the base model's ceiling -- it still improves evidence grounding; and on character 3-gram recall the compact student sometimes surpasses its 70B teacher.

</details>


### [335] [Hierarchical Sparse Attention Done Right: Toward Infinite Context Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02980)
*Xiang Hu,Xinyu Wei,Hao Gu,Minshen Zhang,Tian Liang,Huayang Li,Lei Zhu,Yan Wang,Sirui Han,Yushi Bai,Kewei Tu,Haitao Mi,Leo Liang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HiLS-Attention提出一种分层次块稀疏注意力机制，通过端到端学习块选择，在长上下文中达到甚至超越全注意力的性能，同时支持超过训练长度64倍的外推，并打破了效率与性能的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在处理长上下文时面临全注意力的二次计算复杂度和长度外推能力差的问题。块稀疏注意力虽有潜力，但因块选择不准确而无法匹敌全注意力性能。

Method: 提出分层地标稀疏注意力（HiLS），将注意力分层分解：每个查询独立与每个检索到的块进行注意力计算以提取块特定信息，然后根据块检索分数融合输出。通过将检索分数纳入前向注意力计算，直接使用语言建模损失优化检索分数，实现端到端检索学习和原生稀疏训练。

Result: 在域内上下文长度上达到与全注意力相当甚至更优的性能；支持超过训练长度64倍的外推，检索准确率达90%；现有全注意力模型可通过轻量级继续预训练转换为HiLS模型，保留域内性能的同时获得超长上下文外推能力。

Conclusion: HiLS-Attention通过稀疏KV访问和计算，打破了效率与性能的传统权衡，使长上下文LLM在通用长上下文任务上比全注意力模型更高效且更有效。

Abstract: Scaling modern large language models (LLMs) to long contexts is limited by the quadratic computation cost, and poor length extrapolation of dense attention. Chunk-wise sparse attention offers a promising alternative, but all existing methods fall short of full attention because of their inaccurate chunk selection. We propose Hierarchical Landmark Sparse (HiLS) Attention, a chunk-wise sparse attention mechanism that learns chunk selection end-to-end under the language-modeling (LM) loss. HiLS factorizes attention hierarchically: each query performs attention independently with each retrieved chunk to extract chunk-specific information, and the resulting outputs are fused according to chunk retrieval scores. By incorporating retrieval scores into the forward attention computation, HiLS optimizes them directly with the LM loss, enabling end-to-end retrieval learning and native sparse training. Experimental results show that HiLS-Attention achieves performance comparable to, and in some cases better than, full attention at in-domain context lengths. Meanwhile, HiLS-Attention extrapolates more than $64\times$ the training context length with 90% retrieval accuracy, far beyond full attention. Moreover, existing full-attention models can be converted to HiLS-Attention with lightweight continued pretraining, preserving in-domain performance while acquiring ultra-long-context extrapolation. Together with its sparse KV access and computation, HiLS-Attention breaks the usual efficiency-performance trade-off, enabling long-context LLMs that are both more efficient and more effective on general long-context tasks than their full-attention counterparts.

</details>


### [336] [psytechlab at CLPsych 2026: Utilising Natural Language Processing methods and Large Language Models for Social Media Text Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03003)
*Igor Buyanov,Nafisa Valieva,Ekaterina Mazurina*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文使用LSTM、BERT和LLM等多种NLP方法，在CLPsych 2026共享任务中分析社交媒体帖子的心理健康状态和用户福祉，在摘要任务中取得了排名前列的一致性和矛盾性分数，并在其他任务中取得中等水平结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体帖子是分析心理健康状态的丰富数据源，需要自动化分析工具来评估用户福祉和心理状态，以改进心理健康支持系统。

Method: 采用多种NLP方法，包括LSTM、BERT系列模型以及大型语言模型（LLMs），用于自我状态和福祉分析及摘要生成。

Result: 在CLPsych 2026共享任务的摘要任务中获得了最高的一致性和矛盾性分数之一，在其他任务中取得中等水平结果。

Conclusion: 通过测试和开发心理健康状态估计系统，有助于改进心理健康支持系统；代码已开源。

Abstract: Social media posts are a rich and valuable source of data for analyzing mental health states and users' well-being using automated analysis tools. In this work, we demonstrate how we used a range of Natural Language Processing (NLP) methods, including Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM), BERT-based models, and Large Language Models (LLMs), for self-state and well-being analysis and summarization during the CLPsych Shared Task 2026. Our approach achieved one of the top Consistency and Contradiction scores for the summarization task and also middle-level results for the other tasks. By testing and developing such mental health-state estimation systems, we contributed to improving mental health support systems. We make our code available https://github.com/psytechlab/CLPsych2026/.

</details>


### [337] [Alignment-Guided Largest Table Overlap Size Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03049)
*Ge Lee,Shixun Huang,Zhifeng Bao,Shazia Sadiq,Yanchang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ALORE是一种可扩展且领域鲁棒的表重叠率估计器，通过双视角行列超图编码、对齐引导训练和领域鲁棒值映射，在多个数据集上相比最先进的Armadillo方法MAE降低最高55%，零样本迁移降低69%，同时实现最高89倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的表重叠率估计器Armadillo通过独立编码和嵌入相似度近似重叠率，但在异构存储库中面临三个挑战：(C1)重叠依赖于行列结构，现有编码只能间接推断；(C2)独立编码缺乏表间对齐信号，偏向全局相似度；(C3)朴素值编码过拟合语料特定分布，跨领域性能下降。因此需要一种能显式建模行列结构、引入表间对齐信号且对值分布鲁棒的估计方法。

Method: ALORE基于三个原则：(P1)显式表示行列结构；(P2)训练时暴露表间对齐信号，避免昂贵的对齐搜索；(P3)降低对语料特定值分布的敏感性。具体实现包括：双视角行列超图编码器（Two-View Row-Column Hypergraph encoder）、利用低成本交互信号的对齐引导目标函数、以及领域鲁棒值映射（domain-robust value mapping）。

Result: 在多个跨领域、跨规模的数据集上（包括比先前基准更大的真实世界语料库），ALORE全面超越最先进方法。整体MAE降低最高55%，零样本迁移场景降低69%，同时实现最高89倍的速度提升。在基于表的查询检索任务中也验证了其有效性。

Conclusion: ALORE通过显式建模行列结构、引入对齐引导训练和领域鲁棒值映射，有效解决了现有方法在异构表存储库中重叠率估计不准的问题，在精度和效率上均显著优于Armadillo，且具有良好的跨领域泛化能力。

Abstract: Fast estimation of the size of the largest overlap between tables enables blocking and query-by-table retrieval in large table repositories. The first and the state-of-the-art estimator Armadillo improves efficiency by embedding each table independently and approximating overlap ratio via embedding similarity. However, accurate estimation in heterogeneous repositories remains limited by three challenges: (C1) overlap depends on row-column structure, i.e., each matched cell must preserve both its row and column membership under a joint alignment of the two tables, but existing encodings leave this structure to be inferred indirectly; (C2) independent encoding provides no explicit channel for inter-table alignment signals, biasing prediction toward global similarity; (C3) naive value encodings overfit to corpus-specific distributions, causing cross-domain degradation. Hence, we propose ALORE, a scalable and domain-robust overlap ratio estimator built on three principles: (P1) explicitly represent row-column structure; (P2) expose inter-table alignment signals during training without expensive alignment search; (P3) reduce sensitivity to corpus-specific value distributions. ALORE instantiates these principles with a Two-View Row-Column Hypergraph encoder, alignment-guided objectives with inexpensive interaction signals, and a domain-robust value mapping. Experiments on multiple datasets spanning diverse domains and scales, including a large real-world corpus beyond prior benchmarks, show that ALORE outperforms the state of the art. ALORE reduces MAE by up to 55% overall and 69% in zero-shot transfer, while achieving up to 89x speedup. We further validate its effectiveness for query-by-table retrieval.

</details>


### [338] [Don't Wait to Reply: Towards Responsive yet Thoughtful Dialogue through Proactive Thinking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03093)
*Ante Wang,Jiaqi Fu,Xuanyi Chen,Ruotian Ma,Zhaopeng Tu,Weizhi Ma,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Proactive Thinking框架，让LLM在对话空闲期预计算响应，通过投机性持续思维平衡效率与质量，实现更流畅的实时对话。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM的推理是被动触发的（收到用户输入后才开始思考），导致响应延迟破坏了对话流畅性，与人类在对话间隙主动预判和规划内容的能力形成鲜明对比。

Method: 1. Proactive Thinking框架：在对话空闲期预计算潜在响应元素；2. 训练无关基线：通过投机性持续思维（speculative continual thinking）预判未来状态；3. 将三个不同复杂度的基准适配为时间感知环境，模拟实时对话流。

Result: Proactive Thinking在不牺牲性能的前提下有效提升了交互效率。

Conclusion: 该工作倡导向更智能、更具预判性和实时性的对话AI范式转变。

Abstract: Thinking has emerged as a critical capability for Large Language Models (LLMs) tackling complex tasks. However, its reactive nature, where reasoning is passively triggered only upon receiving a user response, inevitably introduces latency that compromises conversational fluidity. This stands in sharp contrast to human dialogue, where speakers proactively anticipate and plan future content during natural pauses to ensure seamless interaction. To bridge this gap, we propose Proactive Thinking, a framework that empowers models to pre-compute potential response elements during conversational downtime instead of waiting idly for the next input. We then introduce a training-free baseline that can think ahead by anticipating future states, balancing efficiency and quality through speculative continual thinking. To evaluate this approach in practice, we adapt three benchmarks of varying complexity into time-aware environments that simulate real-time conversational flow. We demonstrate that proactive thinking effectively improves interaction efficiency without compromising performance. Ultimately, this work advocates for a fundamental shift toward more intelligent, anticipatory, and real-time conversational AI.

</details>


### [339] [Conditional Diffusion Guided Knowledge Transfer for Multi-Domain Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03154)
*Jiawei Sheng,Taoyu Su,Xixun Lin,Xiaodong Li,Tingwen Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于条件扩散引导的知识迁移框架DMKGC，将多领域知识图谱补全中的每个KG视为实体完整信息的局部视图，通过扩散模型生成领域通用的实体嵌入，在14个KG上平均MRR提升4.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MKGC方法通过对齐跨KG的等价实体施加一致性约束来迁移知识，但这种方法会抑制实体的领域特定上下文信息，损害来自所有KG领域的实体表示信息，尤其在低资源数据场景下阻碍性能提升。

Method: 首次将生成式范式引入MKGC，提出DMKGC框架：(1) 初始化领域无关的实体嵌入作为先验嵌入；(2) 在各KG内分别编码；(3) 融合来自支撑KG的等价实体作为条件扩散生成引导；(4) 利用先验嵌入作为代理生成目标，确保条件生成不对任何条件KG产生偏向；(5) 同时训练生成嵌入使其跨KG具有预测能力以保留领域特定信息。

Result: 在3个基准测试的14个KG上，尾部实体预测平均MRR较最先进方法提升4.3%，在低资源数据场景下也保持持续增益。

Conclusion: 将每个KG视为实体完整信息的局部视图，通过扩散模型生成领域通用嵌入的方法有效克服了传统一致性约束的局限性，验证了生成式范式在MKGC中的有效性。

Abstract: Multi-domain knowledge graph completion (MKGC) aims to improve missing triple prediction in a target KG by transferring knowledge from other support KGs. Existing methods typically enforce consistency constraints on equivalent entities across KGs to transfer knowledge, which risks suppressing domain-specific contextual information of entities. This design can also compromise entity representation information from all KG domains, impeding performance improvements, especially in low-resource data scenarios. To address this, we pioneer a generation-based paradigm for MKGC and propose DMKGC, a conditional diffusion-guided knowledge transfer framework. Our key insight is to treat each KG as a partial view of the entity entire information, and generate informative domain-general entity embeddings through diffusion models conditioned on support KGs. Particularly, we first initialize domain-agnostic entity embeddings as prior entity embeddings, and then encode them within individual KGs. Afterward, we fuse equivalent entities from support KGs as the conditional diffusion generation guidance. We leverage the prior entity embeddings as the proxy generation objective, which ensures this conditional generation to be unbiased towards any conditioned KGs. Simultaneously, we also train the generated embeddings to be predictive across KGs, thus preserving domain-specific information. Extensive experiments on 14 KGs in 3 benchmarks demonstrate a 4.3\% average MRR improvement in tail entity prediction over state-of-the-art methods, with sustained gains in low-resource data settings.

</details>


### [340] [The Role of Prompt Language and Translation-Theory-Driven Prompts in Large Language Models: A Case Study on Spanish-Chinese Journalistic Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03160)
*Haohong Lai,Weijia Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了提示语语言和翻译理论驱动的提示设计如何影响GPT-5.2生成的西班牙语-中文新闻翻译质量。通过4篇《国家报》社论在48种实验条件下的翻译实验，采用BLEU、BERTScore-F1自动评估和基于MQM的人工评估。自动指标显示基线提示(BASE)最佳，而人工评估中简报提示(BRIEF)得分最高(MQM: 8.66 vs 7.84)，这种反转可能源于自动评估的单参考约束。子错误类型分析表明翻译理论驱动的提示能选择性减少Awkward风格错误，但Unidiomatic风格错误在所有条件下持续存在。提示语言影响可忽略不计。结果表明翻译理论驱动的提示在专家评估下能带来可测量的质量提升，但其对语言学习者的教学意义仍需基于用户的研究验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究提示语语言和翻译理论驱动的提示设计如何影响GPT-5.2生成的西班牙语-中文新闻翻译质量，探索优化大语言模型翻译输出的方法。

Method: 构建4篇《国家报》社论的平行语料库，在48种实验条件（4种提示类型×3种提示语言×4篇文章）下进行翻译实验。采用BLEU和BERTScore-F1进行自动评估，基于MQM框架进行人工评估，并进行子错误类型分析。

Result: 自动评估中基线提示(BASE)表现最佳，人工评估中简报提示(BRIEF)得分最高(MQM: 8.66 vs 7.84)。翻译理论驱动的提示能减少Awkward风格错误但无法消除Unidiomatic风格错误，提示语言影响可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 翻译理论驱动的提示在专家评估下能带来可测量的新闻翻译质量提升，但自动评估与人工评估结果存在反转，提示语言影响不显著。对语言学习者的教学意义仍需进一步用户研究验证。

Abstract: This study examines how prompt language and translation theory-driven prompt design influence the quality of Spanish-Chinese journalistic translations generated by GPT-5.2. A parallel corpus of four editorials from El Pais was translated under 48 experimental conditions (4 prompt types, 3 prompt languages, and 4 articles). Translation quality was assessed using BLEU and BERTScore-F1 for automated evaluation, alongside human evaluation based on the Multidimensional Quality Metrics (MQM) framework. Automated metrics identified the baseline prompt (BASE) as the best-performing condition, whereas human evaluation ranked the brief-oriented prompt (BRIEF) highest (MQM: 8.66 vs. 7.84), a reversal likely attributable to the single-reference constraint inherent in automated measures. Sub-error type analysis revealed that translation theory-driven prompts selectively reduced Awkward style errors, while Unidiomatic style errors persisted across conditions. Prompt language had a negligible impact under both evaluation paradigms. These results indicate that translation theory-driven prompts can yield measurable quality gains under expert evaluation of journalistic translations, although their pedagogical implications for language learners remain suggestive and require validation through user-based studies.

</details>


### [341] [KARMA: Knowledge graph-based Automated Reasoning Materialization and Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03166)
*Jinkyeong Choi,Chaebin Jeong,Donghyeon Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对模板对比合成中的分辨率不匹配问题，提出KARMA方法，通过知识图谱枚举schema约束路径并生成槽对齐候选，结合槽并行对齐(SPA)和槽感知掩码注意力，在生物医学、计算机科学和化学基准上优于基线和同类偏好方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模板对比合成可扩展，但其候选样本通常仅在少数实体槽上存在差异，而序列级优化将监督分散在大部分共享模板上，导致分辨率不匹配问题。

Method: 提出KARMA方法，枚举领域知识图谱上的schema约束路径，将其转换为槽对齐对比候选；提出槽并行对齐(SPA)，应用解耦的槽级目标将偏好监督路由到判别性实体槽；槽感知掩码注意力作为可选的打包评估实现。

Result: 在生物医学、计算机科学和化学基准上，KARMA优于基础LLM和同数据SFT基线，并与序列级及token级偏好方法相比表现更优。

Conclusion: KARMA通过槽级对齐有效解决了模板对比合成中的分辨率不匹配问题，在多个科学领域基准上取得了优越性能。

Abstract: Template-based contrastive synthesis is scalable, but its candidates often differ only in a few entity-slots while sequence-level optimization spreads supervision over mostly shared templates. We formalize this as the Resolution Mismatch Problem and propose KARMA, which enumerates schema-constrained paths over domain knowledge graphs and verbalizes them into slot-aligned contrastive candidates. Slot-Parallel Alignment (SPA) then applies a decoupled slot-level objective to route preference supervision to discriminative entity-slots, with slot-aware masked attention serving as an optional packed-evaluation implementation. Across biomedical, computer-science, and chemistry benchmarks, KARMA outperforms base LLM and same-data SFT baselines, and compares favorably with sequence and token-level preference methods.

</details>


### [342] [S-DiverSe: Spanish Diverse Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03207)
*Fernando López,Fernando Ibañez,Ana Martínez,Iván Alonso,Pablo Gómez,Santosh Kesiraju,Jordi Luque*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: S-DiverSe是一个包含3.2小时西班牙语神经性障碍语音的语料库，涵盖22名ALS、帕金森病和中风患者，实验表明启发式文本后处理比微调更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经性疾病影响的语音在自动语音识别中仍面临挑战，缺乏针对西班牙语神经性障碍语音的专用数据集和基准。

Method: 收集22位患有ALS、帕金森病和中风的西班牙语 speakers 的野外语音数据，总计3.2小时，包含444条人工转录音频片段及元数据，并进行了基线ASR实验和微调对比。

Result: 对于域外神经性西班牙语语音，启发式文本后处理比模型微调更鲁棒。

Conclusion: 需要专门针对西班牙语神经性障碍语音的野外基准数据集，简单的后处理方法在当前场景下优于模型微调。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) has advanced remarkably for standard speech, yet speech affected by neurological conditions remains a challenge. We present S-DiverSe (Spanish Diverse Speech), a corpus of 3.2 hours of in-the-wild Spanish speech from 22 speakers with amyotrophic lateral sclerosis, Parkinson's disease, and stroke. The dataset contains 444 manually transcribed audio segments with metadata on speaker sex, disease type, and intelligibility. S-DiverSe is designed to support ASR evaluation and development for neurologically affected Spanish speech. We describe the dataset, analyze its composition, and report baseline ASR results alongside initial adaptation experiments. Our findings reveal that heuristic text post-processing is more robust than fine-tuning for out-of-domain neurological Spanish speech. This underscores the need for dedicated in-the-wild Spanish benchmarks.

</details>


### [343] [TACG: Trajectory-Aware Commit Gating for Diffusion Language Model Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03236)
*Chengcheng Wang,Tingzhang Luo,Wenhao Li,Jianyuan Guo,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TACG，一种无需训练的门控级解码器，通过轨迹感知信号（TILG+历史门控）决定何时提交token，在多个DLLM和基准测试上提升准确率并减少去噪步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散语言模型解码器仅基于当前单步快照提交token，忽略了去噪过程中的预测轨迹，导致置信度与提交就绪性混淆：瞬态峰值可能被错误锁定，而跨步骤持续支持的候选反而延迟。

Method: TACG结合两个组件：(1) Temporal Implicit Logits Guidance (TILG)，对历史logits做指数移动平均作为自参考，并在自然参数空间对比当前logits；(2) History Gate (HG)，要求短期提案持续性才允许提交。配合额外促进预算上限，形成稳定性约束的提交规则，无需辅助网络或额外前向传播。

Result: 在LLaDA、Dream、LLaDA2-Mini上，针对HumanEval、MBPP、GSM8K、MATH500基准测试，TACG通常提升或保持准确率，同时减少去噪步数、增加每次前向传播的token数（TPF）。

Conclusion: TACG证明了利用去噪轨迹的时序信号可以有效改进DLLM的解码效率和质量，且无需训练或额外计算开销。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLLMs) generate text by iteratively denoising masked positions, exposing a trajectory of predictive distributions rather than a single instantaneous belief. Most existing decoders ignore this trajectory and commit tokens from the current snapshot alone, conflating confidence with commitment readiness: a transient top-1 peak under incomplete context can be locked in, while candidates with consistent cross-step support are delayed. We propose Trajectory-Aware Commit Gating (TACG), a training-free gate-level decoder that anchors token identities to the base posterior and uses trajectory-aware signals only to decide whether the current proposal is ready to commit. TACG combines Temporal Implicit Logits Guidance (TILG), which keeps an exponential moving average of past logits as a self-reference and contrasts the current logits against this reference in natural-parameter space, with a History Gate (HG) that enforces short-term proposal persistence before commitment. Together with a capped extra-promotion budget, these components yield a stability-constrained commit rule without auxiliary networks or extra forward passes. We evaluate TACG on LLaDA, Dream, and LLaDA2-Mini across code (HumanEval, MBPP) and math (GSM8K, MATH500) benchmarks; it typically improves or preserves accuracy while reducing denoising steps and increasing tokens per forward (TPF). The code is publicly available at https://github.com/Clarence-CV/TACG-DLLM.

</details>


### [344] [From Gentlemen to Frontiermen: Masculine Formations in English-Language Fiction (1771--1930)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03323)
*Rong Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文利用150部英美经典小说（1771-1930年），通过无监督结构主题模型识别出六种男性气质形态，发现继承性地位和神圣权威的形态衰落，而商业冒险和帝国扩张的形态兴起，其中帝国/冒险男性气质增长最为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 19世纪小说中的男性气质并非单一理想，而是一个充满竞争性脚本的场域。作者旨在量化测量这些竞争性男性权威模式在长期历史进程中的相对 prominence 变化。

Method: 从txtLAB Novel450语料库中选取1771-1930年间150部英美经典小说，使用共指消解将姓名、名词提及和代词归并为人物专属指代链，提取以男性角色为中心的文本窗口，拟合以出版年份和作者性别为主题-流行度协变量的无监督结构主题模型。

Result: 模型识别出六种男性气质形态：贵族-骑士精神、基督教男子气概、绅士体面、乡绅、职业-商业、帝国/冒险。继承性地位和神圣权威形态下降，有偿工作和冒险形态上升，其中帝国/冒险男性气质的增长幅度最大。冒险和商业形态在男性作者的小说中更常见。

Conclusion: 19世纪男性权威发生了从继承性、神圣性地位向获得性、商业性和扩张性形态的重新分配。该研究方法无需种子词汇即可复现，为测量长期性别权威重组提供了可复制的计算路径。

Abstract: Masculinity in nineteenth-century fiction is not a single ideal but a field of competing scripts. Drawing on 150 British and American canonical novels from the txtLAB Novel450 corpus, published between 1771 and 1930, this paper examines the changing relative prominence of competing models of masculine authority. To focus the analysis on masculine characterisation, the study extracts male-character-centred text windows by using coreference resolution to group names, nominal mentions, and pronouns into character-specific reference chains. It then fits an unsupervised structural topic model with publication year and author gender as topic-prevalence covariates. The model identifies six distinct masculine formations: aristocratic-chivalric, Christian manhood, gentlemanly respectability, country squire, professional-commercial, and imperial/adventure. Across the corpus, formations tied to inherited rank and sacred authority decline, while those organised around paid work and adventure rise. The largest increase occurs not in professional-commercial breadwinning but in imperial/adventure masculinity, particularly the frontier-wilderness register. The trajectory points to a reallocation from inherited and sacred status towards achieved, commercial, and expansionary forms of masculine authority. Adventurous and commercial formations are also more prevalent in novels by authors recorded as male. Because these formations emerge without a seeded vocabulary yet align with categories established in independent scholarship, the article offers a reproducible method for measuring the reorganisation of gendered authority across the long nineteenth century.

</details>


### [345] [From Judgments to Issues: Structured Extraction of Legal Reasoning with Citation-Hallucination Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03325)
*Giovanni Piccioli,Alessia Fidelangeli,Piera Santin,Pierpaolo Vivo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一条自动化流水线，将意大利税务法院判决分解为单个法律争议点，并基于IRAC框架和法律三段论为每个争议点提取结构化XML表示；该流水线使用DeepSeek V3模型处理约33万份判决，并耦合了一个自动幻觉检测过滤器以解决法律引用不可靠问题，经50份判决的人工验证，是首个具备幻觉控制的争议级结构化提取流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 税务法院判决数量庞大（约33万份），需要一种自动化方法将非结构化判决文本转换为结构化、可检索的形式，同时解决大语言模型在法律引用方面不可靠的问题。

Method: 构建自动化流水线：使用DeepSeek V3将判决分解为单个法律争议点并提取IRAC/Legal syllogism结构；耦合Linkoln解析器识别引用，经URN-NIR/ECLI/CELEX标准化后与模型输出对比的幻觉检测过滤器；由两位税法博士对50份判决进行标注并计算一致性。

Result: 在50份判决上计算了标注者间一致性和LLM与专家间的一致性，并单独评估了幻觉过滤器的效果；证明了流水线在争议点提取和法律引用方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 这是首个包含幻觉控制、经专家验证的意大利税务判决争议级结构化提取流水线，为下游争议级检索、引用网络分析和大规模法律推理数据集构建提供了基础。

Abstract: We present an automated pipeline that decomposes Italian tax-court judgments into individual legal issues and extracts, for each issue, a structured XML representation grounded in the IRAC framework and the legal syllogism. The pipeline targets a corpus of approximately $330{,}000$ first- and second-instance decisions of the Italian tax courts and is built around a capable yet cost-efficient general-purpose model (DeepSeek V3), a choice driven by the need to process several hundred thousand documents at a sustainable cost. To address the well-documented unreliability of large language models on legal citations, we couple the extraction step with an automatic hallucination-detection filter that compares the references produced by the model with those identified in the judgment text by a dedicated parser (Linkoln), normalised to standard identifiers (URN-NIR, ECLI, CELEX). We validate the pipeline on $50$ judgments annotated by two PhDs in tax law, computing inter-annotator agreement and LLM-vs-expert agreement on both issue extraction and legal citations, together with a stand-alone evaluation of the hallucination filter. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first issue-level, expert-validated structured extraction pipeline with hallucination control for Italian tax-court decisions, and it provides a concrete starting point for downstream applications such as issue-level retrieval, citation-network analysis, and the construction of large-scale datasets of legal reasoning.

</details>


### [346] [Efficient Decentralized Multi-task Dataset Valuation via Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03346)
*Mohammadsajad Alipour,Mohammad Mohammadi Amiri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DMVM提出了一种基于模型合并的去中心化多任务数据集估值框架，通过任务算术在参数空间中推断数据集贡献，避免重训练和数据共享，并支持隐私保护的安全聚合协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集估值方法大多局限于单任务场景，无法处理多任务学习中的贡献评估；同时传统方法（如Shapley值或重训练方法）计算成本高，不适合去中心化环境和严格隐私约束下的数据市场。

Method: 利用任务算术（task arithmetic）直接从模型组合中推断数据集贡献，无需重训练或数据共享；通过参数空间中不同数据集训练的模型的组合方式量化边际效用；引入安全聚合协议支持去中心化部署，保护模型参数和隐私数据。

Result: 在计算机视觉和自然语言处理任务上的综合实验验证了框架有效性；提供了理论误差界来表征近似质量。

Conclusion: DMVM实现了可扩展、计算高效且与多任务泛化行为对齐的数据集估值，为去中心化数据市场中的公平透明定价提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Accurate and efficient dataset valuation is essential for enabling fair and transparent data marketplaces, especially when multiple contributors provide data for training multi-task models. Most existing valuation methods, however, are limited to single-task settings, overlooking scenarios where a buyer aims to optimize performance across multiple downstream tasks. Moreover, traditional valuation approaches, such as Shapley-based or retraining-based methods, are computationally expensive and poorly suited for decentralized environments without a trusted central coordinator and with strict privacy constraints. We propose DMVM (Decentralized Multi-task Valuation via Model Merging), a novel framework that bypasses retraining and data sharing by leveraging task arithmetic to infer dataset contributions directly from model combinations. Instead of retraining or sharing raw data, DMVM quantifies how models trained on different datasets combine in parameter space to infer each dataset's marginal utility across multiple tasks. This formulation yields a valuation process that is scalable, computationally efficient, and explicitly aligned with multi-task generalization behavior. To support decentralized deployment, we introduce a secure aggregation protocol that enables collaborative valuation without revealing individual model parameters or private data. We also provide theoretical error bounds characterizing the approximation quality of DMVM and validate our framework through comprehensive experiments on computer vision and natural language processing tasks.

</details>


### [347] [Spectral Signatures of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03377)
*Zhuoying Zhang,Ishan V. Prasad,Yuanzhe Hu,Zihang Liu,Hengrui Luo,Pu Ren,Yaoqing Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于重尾自正则化理论的谱形状度量，利用权重经验谱密度作为大型语言模型的紧凑谱特征，支持无数据、高效且尺度不变的模型管理、溯源、聚类和性能量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公开可用的LLM数量快速增长，缺乏系统化的管理方法（如模型溯源、许可管理、评估），而任务特定基准因模型架构、规模和训练过程的差异而不足以应对大规模分析需求。

Method: 基于重尾自正则化理论，采用权重经验谱密度的形状信息作为每个模型的紧凑谱特征。该特征捕获预训练模型的内在属性，且在训练后保持鲁棒性，具有无数据、计算高效和尺度不变的特点。

Result: 在涵盖主要开源LLM家族的大规模模型语料库上，谱特征支持模型溯源追踪、无监督聚类相似模型以及量化模型性能。作为广泛性能趋势的有意义代理指标，实现了高效的大规模模型组织、比较和分析。

Conclusion: 所提出的谱特征为LLM的大规模系统化管理提供了有效工具，能够在无需下游任务数据的情况下进行模型级分析，具有良好的可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: The rapidly growing repository of publicly available large language models (LLMs) presents significant challenges for systematic management and quantification at scale, such as model lineage tracing, licensing, and evaluation. However, task-specific benchmarks are insufficient for this setting, as LLMs differ widely in architectures, scales, and training procedures. To address this challenge, we adopt spectral shape-based metrics for managing and quantifying LLMs based on Heavy-Tailed Self-Regularization theory. Our approach uses the shape information of the weight empirical spectral density as a compact spectral signature of each model. This signature captures intrinsic properties of pretrained models and remains robust during post-training, making it suitable for model-level analysis. In addition, this metric is data-free, computationally-efficient, and scale-invariant, enabling large-scale analysis in practice. Moreover, we curate a large and diverse model corpus consisting of major open-source LLM families, and use it to systematically benchmark spectral and non-spectral metrics across models and downstream tasks. We show that our spectral signature supports the tracking of the model lineage, the unsupervised clustering of similar models, and the quantification of the model performance. Overall, the proposed spectral signature provides a meaningful proxy for broad performance trends across LLMs, enabling efficient organization, comparison, and analysis of large model collections.

</details>


### [348] [The Classics at SemEval-2026 Task 3: Combining Transformer Models and LLM-Generated Annotations for Dimensional Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03414)
*Rafif Alshawi,Amit Raj,Aleksey Kudelya,Alexander Shirnin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于SemEval-2026 Task 3的维度情感分析方法，通过集成Transformer模型进行回归预测（情感效价与唤醒度），并使用解码器LLM进行结构化预测以同时提取情感元素及其数值分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分类情感分析仅提供离散标签（如正面/负面），缺乏对情感细腻程度的刻画。本文旨在突破这一局限，实现连续、多维度的情感数值预测。

Method: 对于回归子任务（Subtask 1），采用加权集成的Transformer编码器模型；针对俄语，使用LLM生成合成情感描述以增强输入。对于提取子任务（Subtask 3），微调解码器LLM进行结构化预测，同时识别情感元素（方面、类别、观点）并估计其数值分数。

Result: 参与两个子任务：Subtask 1（预测情感效价和唤醒度的数值分数）和Subtask 3（提取完整情感细节集合，包括方面、类别、观点及其分数）。

Conclusion: 所提方法能够有效实现从离散分类到连续数值的情感分析范式转变，集成模型与LLM微调分别在回归和结构化提取任务上展现了可行性与有效性。

Abstract: This paper presents an approach to the SemEval-2026 Task 3: Dimensional Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis. We investigate methods for moving beyond traditional categorical sentiment (e.g., positive or negative) to predict fine-grained, real-valued scores for sentiment "valence" (positivity) and "arousal" (intensity). We participate in two subtasks: predicting these scores for given aspects (Subtask 1) and extracting full sets of sentiment details, including aspects, categories, and opinions alongside their scores (Subtask 3). Our approach for the regression task involves a weighted ensemble of transformer-based encoder models. For the Russian language, we further enhance the input by using a large language model (LLM) to generate synthetic sentiment descriptions. For the extraction task, we fine-tune a decoder LLM to perform structured prediction, allowing the system to identify sentiment elements and estimate their numerical scores simultaneously.

</details>


### [349] [CaresAI at SMM4H-HeaRD 2026: Predicting TNM Staging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03466)
*Joseph Itopa Abubakar,Jorge Jarme,Favour Igwezeke,Mary Adewunmi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究针对SMM4H-HeaRD 2026第六个共享任务，使用TCGA病理报告独立预测TNM分期标签。采用TF-IDF和ClinicalBERT、BioBERT、PubMedBERT嵌入，结合LR、LightGBM、FFNN、WRN等模型进行多标签分类。WRN在训练阶段取得了T:0.839、N:0.8502、M:0.803的AUROC；LightGBM+TF-IDF表现最佳，AUROC达T:0.9368、N:0.9524、M:0.8311。测试集1 Macro-F1为T:0.978、N:0.957、M:0.879；测试集2为T:0.807、N:0.767、M:1.0。但评估阶段性能下降，表明模型泛化性、类别不平衡和长文档处理存在局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用TCGA病理报告独立预测TNM分期标签，为癌症分期自动化提供基线模型。

Method: 将问题构建为三个多标签分类任务，使用TF-IDF和ClinicalBERT、BioBERT、PubMedBERT嵌入，采用LR、LightGBM、FFNN、WRN四种分类器。

Result: WRN在训练集AUROC为T:0.839、N:0.8502、M:0.803；LightGBM+TF-IDF最佳，AUROC为T:0.9368、N:0.9524、M:0.8311。测试集1 Macro-F1为T:0.978、N:0.957、M:0.879；测试集2为T:0.807、N:0.767、M:1.0。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了有效基线和可复现流程，但模型泛化性不足、对类别不平衡敏感、处理长文档存在挑战，需进一步优化验证才能用于临床。

Abstract: This study aims to predict Tumor, Node, and Metastasis (TNM) stage labels independently, with the Cancer Genome Atlas (TCGA) pathology report as the sixth shared task of SMM4H-HeaRD 2026. The problem is framed as three multi-label classification tasks. We explore both classical and deep learning approaches using Term Frequency-Inverse Document Frequency (TF-IDF) features and embeddings from ClinicalBERT, BioBERT, and PubMedBERT. These representations are used with Logistic Regression (LR), Light Gradient Boosting Machine (LightGBM), Feed-Forward Neural Networks (FFNN), and Wide Residual Networks (WRN). Our results show that individual embeddings perform similarly to the TNM label classification, while their combination improves its predictive ability. WRN achieves AUROC scores of 0.839 (T), 0.8502 (N), and 0.803 (M) with F1-scores of 0.622, 0.702, and 0.9337, respectively, for the training phase. LightGBM with TF-IDF performs best with AUROC scores of 0.9368 (T), 0.9524 (N), and 0.8311 (M) and F1-scores of 0.7559 (T), 0.7384 (N), and 0.7017 (M) during the training phase. Furthermore, the result of the Codabench for the test sets indicates a Macro-F1 score of 0.978, 0.957, and 0.879 for the T, N, and M categories respectively for test set 1; while test set 2 records a Macro-F1 score for T, N, and M is 0.807, 0.767, 1.0 respectively. However, performance declined during the evaluation phase of the test sets, a drop from 0.938 to 0.858 of test set 1 to 2, for the Macro-F1 score across all stages; suggesting limitations in model generalizability, sensitivity to class imbalance, and challenges in processing lengthy clinical documents. Although this study provides an efficient baseline model and a reproducible pipeline, further optimization and validation are required before it can be considered suitable for use in a real-world clinical setting.

</details>


### [350] [Learning from Lost Provenance: Multiple Instance Learning for Cancer Registry Tumor Group Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03481)
*Leonard Ruocco,Jonathan Simkin,Lovedeep Gondora,Gregory Arbour,Raymond Ng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出利用注意力机制的多实例学习（ABMIL）框架，将癌症登记处日常运营中产生的患者级标签转化为高质量的报告级训练数据，无需额外人工标注，实现了对病理报告肿瘤分组的自动分类，宏F1达0.83。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癌症登记处依赖深度学习自动化病理报告编码，但缺乏报告级人工标注训练数据。尽管登记处运营中会产生大量专家标注的标签，但这些标签存在于患者层面，无法关联到具体的病理报告，限制了其直接用于模型训练。

Method: 采用注意力机制的多实例学习（ABMIL）框架，利用患者级标签与病理报告之间的关联，通过模型对每份报告的注意力权重，从大量带有噪声标签的数据集中提取出紧凑、高质量的报告级训练数据，再用该蒸馏数据集微调分类器。

Result: 在不列颠哥伦比亚癌症登记处的肿瘤分组分类任务中，经蒸馏数据集微调的分类器宏F1达到0.83，在大多数肿瘤分组上优于已有基线方法。

Conclusion: ABMIL框架能够将常规运营标签转化为高质量训练数据，无需额外标注或大规模计算基础设施，为癌症登记工作流程的自动化提供了一条实用且可及的路径。

Abstract: Modernizing cancer registries with deep learning is opening new opportunities to automate labor-intensive tasks such as the coding of pathology reports. However, progress is constrained by the scarcity of report-level human-annotated training data. Cancer registries generate substantial volumes of expert-assigned labels as a routine product of their operations, but these exist at the patient level and are not linked to the individual pathology reports that informed them, limiting their direct use for training models. We develop an efficient framework for training deep learning classifiers by leveraging these operationally-generated labels without requiring per-report human annotation, demonstrated for tumor group classification at the BC Cancer Registry. We use Attention-Based Multiple Instance Learning (ABMIL) to recover the lost link between patient-level labels and the reports that informed them, leveraging the attention the model places on each report to distil a large, noisily-labeled corpus into a compact, high-quality per-report training dataset. A classifier fine-tuned on a distilled dataset achieved a macro F1 of 0.83, outperforming established baselines across most tumor groups. By turning routine operational labels into high-quality training data without additional annotation or large-scale computing infrastructure, ABMIL offers a practical and accessible route to automating cancer registry workflows.

</details>


### [351] [Lacuna Inc. at SemEval-2026 Task 4: Structurally Gated State-Space Models for Disentangling Narrative Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.03482)
*Aleksey Kudelya,Rafif Alshawi,Alexander Shirnin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出IVD-SSM模型，通过混合状态空间模型结合结构门控对齐头，将叙事中的结构不变特征与词汇变体特征解耦，在SemEval-2026任务4的成对比较和密集表征学习两项评测中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 叙事相似度评估需要模型超越具体名称、角色、场景等表层元素，捕捉抽象的因果模式和情节推进结构，而标准Transformer在建模长程因果链时存在二次方复杂度瓶颈。

Method: 采用混合状态空间模型Jamba-1.5-Mini作为骨干网络，提出Structurally Gated Alignment (SGA) head：大跨度Macro路径提取故事粗粒度结构骨架，作为门控机制过滤全分辨率Micro路径，抑制语义噪声和表层关键词重叠，实现结构不变特征与词汇变体特征的显式解耦。

Result: 在Track A（成对比较判断）和Track B（密集表征学习）两项任务上均取得了有效结果，证明解耦策略为深层叙事理解提供了稳健框架。

Conclusion: 显式解耦结构不变特征与词汇变体特征是实现深层叙事理解的有效原则性方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we present the Invariant-Variant Disentangled State-Space Model (IVD-SSM), our submission to SemEval-2026 Task 4 on Narrative Story Similarity and Narrative Representation Learning. Evaluating narrative similarity is a profound computational challenge that requires models to look past concrete, superficial elements such as specific names, actors, objects, or settings to isolate and compare abstract patterns of causality and plot progression. To model these extended causal chains without the quadratic bottlenecks of standard Transformers, we leverage a hybrid State-Space Model (Jamba-1.5-Mini). Building upon this backbone, we introduce the Structurally Gated Alignment (SGA) head, a novel, differentiable algorithmic architecture.
